Result: SNIPER的CRAFT分数达0.98，近乎精确满足压缩比率，而现有剪枝器偏离目标压缩比率高达33%。在四个架构、18个任务（覆盖五个领域）的评估中，SNIPER在平均性能保持和任务级稳定性上均优于六个SOTA剪枝器，平均排名1.25。

Conclusion: SNIPER展示了跨架构的泛化能力和出色的可靠性，以背包优化替代贪婪启发式，能够同时兼顾压缩预算的精确性和模型性能的保留，是结构化剪枝领域的一种稳健且有效的方案。

Abstract: Structured pruning is a promising approach for compressing large language models (LLMs), yet existing methods rely heavily on greedy heuristics that produce myopic decisions, and often fail to precisely meet target compression budgets. We present SNIPER, a two-stage structured pruning framework that solves a knapsack optimization over coarse-granularity components to yield conditionally optimal parameter allocations with respect to fixed importance estimates, followed by a fine-grained pruning stage to meet strict budget constraints. We introduce the Compression Ratio Adherence Factor (CRAFT) to quantify budget fidelity, showing that while existing pruners deviate from target compression ratios by up to 33%, SNIPER achieves near-exact adherence with a CRAFT score of 0.98. Evaluations across four diverse architectures over a set of 18 tasks spanning five domains demonstrate SNIPER's consistent improvements in average performance retention and task-level stability over six state-of-the-art pruners. Across all pruning configurations, SNIPER achieves an excellent mean rank of 1.25, indicating its robust cross-architectural generalizability and excellent reliability.

</details>


### [139] [LycheeMemory V2: Efficient Long-Term Memory for LLM Agents via Semantic Segment-Level Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12990)
*Dongfang Li,Zixuan Liu,Junmai Wang,Jiahe Huang,Fuhao Li,Bonian Jia,Baotian Hu,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LycheeMemory V2是一个高效的长时记忆框架，采用语义段级整合替代传统的回合级整合，通过批量编码交换片段和语义边界检测，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低记忆构建成本，在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上达到SOTA性能并减少高达86%的构建token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长时记忆系统依赖急切的逐回合整合（每次交互后都调用LLM提取、总结或更新记忆），导致对话增长时记忆构建成本越来越高。粗粒度总结虽可降低成本但会丢失细粒度上下文证据，而扩大检索上下文或多跳LLM推理则把开销转移到查询阶段。因此需要一种既能控制构建成本又不牺牲检索质量的记忆框架。

Method: LycheeMemory V2将回合级整合替换为语义段级整合：将多个交换批量组成段落，对每个已完成段落编码为上下文无关的typed memory records。段级批处理降低LLM编码频率，语义边界检测相比固定窗口批处理能更好保留连贯的事件级和时间证据。这些记录通过轻量级结构化索引进行组织，支持查询计划的证据检索。

Result: 使用GPT-4.1-Mini实验，LycheeMemory在LoCoMo上达到89.22%，在LongMemEval-S上达到92.20%，均为SOTA性能。与A-Mem相比，在LoCoMo上减少86.0%的构建token，在LongMemEval-S上减少75.9%，且不增加查询阶段的token使用量。

Conclusion: 长期智能体记忆的准确率-成本权衡不仅取决于保留哪些信息，还取决于信息整合的粒度。语义段级整合提供了一个更优的平衡点，在显著降低构建成本的同时保持甚至提升检索性能。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents must preserve information from past interactions to support future tasks. Existing memory systems typically rely on eager consolidation, invoking LLMs after each interaction to extract, summarize, or update memories. This design makes memory construction increasingly costly as conversations grow. Coarse summarization can reduce construction cost but risks discarding fine-grained contextual evidence, whereas larger retrieval contexts or multi-hop LLM reasoning shift the overhead to query time. We present LycheeMemory V2, an efficient long-term memory framework that replaces turn-level consolidation with semantic segment-level consolidation. Instead of consolidating every interaction, LycheeMemory batches multiple exchanges into segments and encodes each finalized segment into context-independent typed memory records. Segment-level batching lowers LLM encoding frequency, while semantic boundary detection helps preserve coherent event-level and temporal evidence compared with fixed-window batching. The resulting records are organized with lightweight structured indexes for query-planned evidence retrieval. Experiments using GPT-4.1-Mini show that LycheeMemory achieves state-of-the-art performance, reaching 89.22% on LoCoMo and 92.20% on LongMemEval-S. Compared with A-Mem, it reduces construction tokens by 86.0% on LoCoMo and 75.9% on LongMemEval-S without increasing query-time token usage. More broadly, our results suggest that the accuracy--cost trade-off of long-term agent memory depends not only on what information is retained, but also on the granularity at which it is consolidated.

</details>


### [140] [HybridRAG-BN: A Retrieval-Augmented Framework with Fine-Tuned Verification for Bangla KBQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13004)
*Rathijit Aich,Nirjhar Das,Mahfuzulhoq Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出HybridRAG-BN，一个面向孟加拉语知识库问答（KBQA）的检索增强生成框架，结合BM25和BGE-M3的混合检索、GGUF版Gemma-4-31B-Instruct的答案生成、以及LoRA微调模型进行答案验证与精炼，并加入后处理阶段处理未解决案例。在公共和私有排行榜上分别取得0.71654和0.72912的token级F1分数，荣获竞赛第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉语等低资源语言的KBQA系统面临检索研究匮乏、语言资源稀缺、生成回答难以在外部知识中落地等挑战，亟需一个可靠的检索增强推理框架来提升低资源语言KBQA的性能。

Method: 1) 混合检索：结合BM25稀疏检索与BGE-M3稠密检索；2) 答案生成：使用GGUF量化的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct生成回答；3) 验证与精炼：利用LoRA微调的Gemma-4-31B-Instruct模型对生成答案进行验证和修正；4) 后处理：通过fallback答案替换和DuckDuckGo辅助检索处理未解决案例。

Result: 在公共排行榜上取得0.71654的token级F1分数，在私有排行榜上取得0.72912的F1分数，获得竞赛第一名。

Conclusion: HybridRAG-BN通过整合混合检索、生成、验证后处理的多阶段流水线，有效解决了孟加拉语KBQA面临的知识检索和答案接地问题，验证了检索增强框架在低资源语言场景下的可行性和优越性。

Abstract: Knowledge-base question answering (KBQA) systems rely on effective retrieval and reasoning mechanisms to generate accurate answers from external knowledge sources. However, developing reliable KBQA systems for low-resource languages such as Bangla remains challenging due to limited retrieval-focused research, scarce language resources, and difficulties in grounding generated responses in external knowledge. In this work, we propose HybridRAG-BN, a retrieval-augmented framework for Bangla KBQA that integrates hybrid retrieval using BM25 and BGE-M3, answer generation using the GGUF version of Gemma-4-31B-Instruct, and a LoRA-fine-tuned Gemma-4-31B-Instruct model for answer verification and refinement. To further improve robustness, the framework incorporates a post-processing stage that addresses unresolved cases through fallback answer replacement and DuckDuckGo-assisted retrieval. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework, achieving token-level F1 scores of 0.71654 and 0.72912 on the public and private leaderboards, respectively, securing first place in the competition.

</details>


### [141] [EviReform: Evidence-Guided Query Reformulation for Multi-Hop Graph Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13006)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EviReform是一种新的多跳检索方法，通过将检索请求修正与图证据聚合分离，利用已检索到的源段落生成残差查询来弥补原始问题中未明确的信息需求，在三个多跳问答数据集上超越了最强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多跳检索需要恢复能共同提供充分证据的段落，但初始段落常常隐含地解析了问题中的实体或关系，导致缺失证据只有在检索开始后才更容易描述。传统图检索的检索信号通常来自原始问题，即使观察到更直接的语义线索，也必须通过存储的关系来到达补充证据，效率低下。

Method: EviReform将修订检索请求与图证据聚合分离。检索到的源段落为未解决的信息需求制定残差查询，然后将原始和残差检索信号分别归一化、组合，并在共享实体的命题之间传播。

Result: 在2WikiMultiHopQA、HotpotQA和MuSiQue数据集上，EviReform比最强基线最高提升5.59个Recall@5点和4.50个F1点。

Conclusion: 观察到的证据可以引导图检索朝向原始问题未充分指定的支持链部分，证明了从检索过程中获取的语义线索能够有效补充原始查询的不足。

Abstract: Multi-hop retrieval must recover passages that provide sufficient evidence together. An initial passage often resolves an entity or relation implicit in the question, making the missing evidence easier to describe only after retrieval begins. Graph retrieval improves access to related evidence through stored corpus structure, but its retrieval signal is commonly derived from the original question. Complementary evidence must then be reached through stored relations even when an observed passage provides a more direct semantic cue. We introduce EviReform, which separates revising the retrieval request from aggregating evidence in the graph. Retrieved source passages formulate residual queries for the unresolved information need. The original and residual retrieval signals are normalized separately, combined, and propagated between propositions that share entities. On 2WikiMultiHopQA, HotpotQA, and MuSiQue, EviReform exceeds the strongest baseline by up to 5.59 Recall@5 points and 4.50 F1 points. These results show that observed evidence can guide graph retrieval toward the part of a supporting chain left underspecified by the original question. Code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/EviReform.

</details>


### [142] [RAGSieve: Self-Referenced Local Contrast for Knowledge-Poison Detection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13010)
*Xinlong Xu,Yoshua Y. Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RAGSieve,一个无需可信语料库和中毒标签的自引用检测框架,用于检测检索增强生成(RAG)系统中的投毒攻击。包含查询本地对比(RSQ)和语料库本地对比(RSG)两个模块,分别在查询时和语料库摄入时提供保护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统将外部语料库作为推理证据,攻击者可通过注入文档来推广其选定的主张。现有检测方法依赖可信参考、特定攻击特征或对语料库拓扑敏感的全局阈值,存在明显局限性,需要更鲁棒的检测方案。

Method: RAGSieve包含两个核心组件:1)RSQ(查询本地对比):对同一检索结果中的前5个候选与第6-20位排名进行对比,检测答案锚点集中度和载体转换;2)RSG(语料库本地对比):比较每个文档的语义相似但词汇不同的邻居与其局部基线,在查询到达前检测协同密度异常。

Result: 在三个QA数据集和六种投毒构造下,RSQ达到95.2% AUROC,在5%干净文档移除下检测82.2%的中毒(对比GMTP的81.1%/52.5%);RSG达到93.3%/79.8%(对比CleanBase的79.4%/37.6%)。联合部署将攻击成功率从67.4%降至14.0%,同时在未中毒检索上保持41.3% F1。

Conclusion: RAGSieve在语料库摄入和查询两个阶段均提供了实用的投毒防护能力,无需中毒标签或可信语料库,显著优于现有基线方法,实现了攻击成功率的有效降低。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation treats an external corpus as inference evidence, allowing injected documents to promote attacker-chosen claims. Existing detectors depend on trusted references, specific attack artifacts, or global thresholds sensitive to corpus topology. We present RAGSieve, a self-referenced detection framework that constructs its reference from the inspected system. RAGSieve-Query (RSQ) performs query-local contrast, scoring top-five candidates against ranks 6-20 of the same retrieval to detect answer-anchor concentration and carrier transitions. RAGSieve-Graph (RSG) performs corpus-local contrast, comparing each document's semantically similar but lexically distinct neighbors with its local baseline to detect coordinated density before queries arrive. Across three QA datasets and six poisoning constructions, RSQ achieves 95.2% AUROC and detects 82.2% of poison at 5% clean-document removal, versus 81.1%/52.5% for GMTP. RSG achieves 93.3%/79.8%, versus 79.4%/37.6% for CleanBase. Joint deployment reduces attack success from 67.4% to 14.0% while retaining 41.3% F1 on unpoisoned retrieval, demonstrating practical protection at both corpus ingestion and query time without poison labels or trusted corpora. Source code is available at https://github.com/XrazyMee/RAGSieve.

</details>


### [143] [CASA: Content-Acoustic Speaking Assessment with Speech Encoder and Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13101)
*Nhan Phan,Ilona Lähteenmäki,Anna von Zansen,Olli-Pekka Pauna,Yaroslav Getman,Tamás Grósz,Mikko Kurimo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CASA，一种结合Whisper-medium和Qwen3.5-2B的简单架构，在自动口语评估（ASA）中达到最先进性能（RMSE 0.358），同时提供更可解释的语音表达与内容分离，并通过消融实验和重复运行分析声学与内容信息的贡献及性能稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多模态语音大语言模型的自动口语评估研究，对声学信息和内容信息如何贡献于预测的分析有限，且对性能稳定性的研究不足。

Method: 提出CASA架构，结合Whisper-medium（处理声学特征）和Qwen3.5-2B（处理内容信息），使用三个手工设计的流利度特征，实现语音表达与内容的可解释分离，架构通用性强，可直接适配其他ASA语料库。

Result: 在Speak & Improve Corpus 2025上，CASA达到RMSE 0.358，优于此前最佳结果，同时推理参数约为之前方法的一半。通过消融实验和重复运行，分析了声学与内容信息的独立及互补贡献，考察了性能变异性，并展示了LLM推理在无需训练的内容验证方面的潜力。

Conclusion: CASA证明了更简单的架构可以在ASA中取得更好性能，同时提供更清晰的模型可解释性，并为理解声学与内容信息在口语评估中的各自角色提供了实证分析。

Abstract: Research on automatic speaking assessment (ASA) has increasingly adopted multimodal speech large language models to assess learners' speaking performance. However, existing studies provide limited analysis of how acoustic and content information contribute to predictions and how stable the resulting performance is. We propose CASA, a simpler architecture combining Whisper-medium and Qwen3.5-2B that achieves state-of-the-art performance while providing a more interpretable separation between speech delivery and content.
  On the Speak & Improve Corpus 2025, CASA achieves a root mean square error (RMSE) of 0.358, improving on the previous best RMSE while using approximately half the estimated inference parameters. The general-purpose architecture is designed for adaptation to other ASA corpora without structural changes and relies on three handcrafted fluency features. Through ablations and repeated runs, we analyze the individual and complementary contributions of acoustic and content information, examine performance variability, and demonstrate the potential of large language model reasoning for training-free content validation.

</details>


### [144] [LigBench: A Unified and Human-Aligned Benchmark for LLM-based Research Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13136)
*Chenrun Wang,Mingxuan Zhu,Tiancheng Huang,Wenjie Li,Yujie Zhang,Zichen Zhu,Zhiying Zou,Kai Yu,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LigBench，一个自动化基准测试，用于细粒度且可靠地评估AI研究想法，同时引入PAIR-IQ数据集训练配对想法判断模型，以支持更客观的比较评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究想法生成评估方法碎片化，缺乏客观标准，往往依赖直接的LLM评分，无法在生成想法的连贯分布上提供统一可靠的评估。

Method: 提出LigBench（自动化评估基准）和PAIR-IQ（配对想法判断模型训练数据集）。LigBench可实现细粒度、可靠的评估并适用于不同生成分布；PAIR-IQ作为辅助参考支持更客观的比较评估。

Result: 实验表明LigBench能够实现稳定且可解释的评估，显著提升与专家判断的一致性；基于PAIR-IQ训练的模型在排序准确性和鲁棒性方面表现更佳。

Conclusion: 该工作为可扩展的、客观的研究想法评估建立了原则性标准，填补了该领域评估框架的空白。

Abstract: With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), research idea generation has attracted increasing attention. Existing approaches enable LLMs to retrieve relevant literature and propose novel ideas for research areas. However, current evaluation practices for idea generation remain fragmented and lack objective standards, often relying on direct LLM scoring, which limits their ability to provide unified and reliable assessments across a coherent distribution of generated ideas. To address this challenge, we propose LigBench, an automated evaluation benchmark that enables fine-grained and reliable evaluation of AI research ideas, consistently applicable across different generation distributions. In addition, we introduce PAIR-IQ, a dataset tailored for training pairwise idea judgment models and serving as an auxiliary reference to support more objective comparative evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LigBench achieves stable and interpretable evaluations, significantly improving alignment with expert judgments. Furthermore, models trained on PAIR-IQ exhibit enhanced ranking accuracy and robustness, establishing a principled standard for scalable and objective research idea assessment.

</details>


### [145] [Better Decomposition, Free Aggregation: A Synthesizer-Folding Framework for Multilingual Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13160)
*Yilin Wang,Yuchun Fan,Weidong Bao,Zili Wei,Shi Feng,Tong Xiao,Zhengtao Yu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Syfer框架，用于多语言多跳问答的检索增强生成。Syfer通过延迟翻译策略、子问题图分解及质量检查机制，避免了传统方法中一刀切翻译带来的信息损失和开销，以及贪婪分解导致的错误累积，在保持竞争力的同时实现了性能与计算成本的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mRAG方法存在两大局限：(1) 统一翻译对齐会丢失目标语言特有的文化和语言原生信息，引入翻译噪声并增加系统成本；(2) 贪婪的分解与聚合会产生冗余子问题，在逐步推理中放大错误。

Method: Syfer采用合成器折叠框架。首先通过格式约束的分解器生成原始语言的子问题图，并进行分解质量检查；检查通过后按检索-回答策略用目标语言顺序回答子问题；仅在检查失败时才激活英文翻译路径并进行双语子问题图对齐。

Result: 跨多语言的实验表明，Syfer在保持竞争性准确率的同时，在性能与计算成本之间取得了良好平衡。

Conclusion: 延迟翻译而非默认翻译的策略是有效的：通过引入分解质量检查和条件性翻译，可以在降低计算成本的同时保持高准确率。

Abstract: Multilingual retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) equips large language models with access to globally distributed external knowledge for complex multilingual question answering. Recent approaches either translate retrieved documents into English or the query language to bridge the cross-lingual semantic gap, or decompose a complex query into sub-questions and aggregate the intermediate reasoning process. However, both lines of work suffer from two limitations. First, one-size-fits-all translation alignment, blanket translation discards culturally and linguistically native information unique to the target language, introduces translation noise, and inflates system cost. Second, greedy decomposition and aggregation, uncontrolled decomposition produces redundant sub-questions that compound errors during step-wise reasoning, and the final aggregation over reasoning paths further amplifies these errors. We address both with our method Syfer, a synthesizer-folding framework for multilingual multi-hop question answering that defers translation rather than applying it by default. Syfer first invokes a format-constrained decomposer to produce a sub-question graph in the original language, followed by a decomposition-quality check; when the check passes, sub-questions are answered sequentially under a retrieve-then-answer policy in the target language, and the English translation pathway with bilingual sub-question graph alignment is activated only when the check fails. Experiments across multiple languages show that Syfer attains competitive accuracy while striking a favourable balance between performance and computational cost.

</details>


### [146] [Which LLM Is Your Ideal Companion? Evaluating Emotional Companion Capabilities of LLMs Based on Adult Attachment Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13168)
*Junkai Zhou,Shiting Guan,Zhaoyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入成人依恋理论，使用ECR-R量表评估LLM在亲密关系中的依恋焦虑与回避倾向，并提出情感陪伴基准ECBench，涵盖情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决和社交引导四个场景，通过11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法对32个LLM进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多地用于情感陪伴，但现有评估主要刻画一般人格特质，缺乏对亲密关系和情感敏感情境中模型行为的洞察，因此需要引入心理学理论视角进行专门评估。

Method: 引入成人依恋理论，利用ECR-R量表刻画依恋焦虑与回避；构建ECBench情感陪伴基准，覆盖友谊与浪漫关系下的四个场景（情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决、社交引导），采用11个对话质量指标和三种评估方法。

Result: 评估了32个LLM的依恋倾向，选择代表性模型探究这些倾向在多轮交互中的表现，并验证是否可通过提示词塑造模型行为。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解和选择用于情感陪伴的LLM提供了心理学理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied for emotional companionship, evaluating their behavior and capabilities in intimate relationships has become a pressing issue. However, existing assessments primarily characterize general personality traits, providing limited insight into model behavior within intimate and emotionally sensitive contexts. Therefore, we introduce adult attachment theory into LLM evaluation and use the Experiences in Close Relationships-Revised (ECR-R) scale to characterize attachment anxiety and avoidance. To evaluate emotional companionship capabilities of LLMs in realistic interaction scenarios, we present an emotional companionship benchmark, ECBench, spanning four scenarios including emotional support, collaborative tasks, conflict resolution, and social guidance, across friendship and romantic relationships. ECBench is utilized to assess model behavior using 11 dialogue-quality metrics and three evaluation methods. We evaluate the attachment tendencies of 32 LLMs and select representative models to investigate how these tendencies manifest in contextualized multi-turn interactions and whether they can be shaped through prompting. Our study provides a theoretical lens from psychology, along with practical tools to understand and select LLMs for emotional companionship.

</details>


### [147] [GEM: A Generative Embedding Model Bridging Reasoning and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13200)
*Zhili Shen,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GEM，一种生成式嵌入模型，通过显式推理用户意图和相关性标准来增强检索能力。GEM在同一模型中统一生成与嵌入，先对查询进行推理，再附加嵌入token编码增强后的上下文用于检索。实验表明GEM在推理密集型和指令跟随检索任务上优于非推理变体，并能匹敌使用更大模型的基线；其生成特性还支持通过提示进行测试时计算扩展以进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代LLM擅长推理和指令跟随，用户能表达复杂多样的信息需求，但传统检索器主要依赖查询与文档的表面匹配，导致用户需求表达方式与检索器理解方式之间存在日益扩大的差距。

Method: 提出GEM（Generative Embedding Model），将生成与嵌入统一在单一模型中：先对查询进行显式推理（理解用户意图和相关性标准），然后附加一个嵌入token来编码增强后的上下文用于检索。

Result: 在推理密集型和指令跟随检索任务上，GEM优于其非推理变体，并能匹敌使用显著更大模型基线的性能；通过提示进行测试时计算扩展可进一步提升检索性能。

Conclusion: 推理增强的检索范式可以有效弥合用户表达与检索器理解之间的差距，且生成式架构天然支持测试时计算扩展以进一步增强检索效果。

Abstract: Modern LLMs excel at reasoning and instruction following, enabling users to express complex and diverse information needs. However, conventional retrievers largely rely on surface-level matching between queries and documents, resulting in a growing gap between how users express their needs and how retrievers interpret them. In this paper, we present GEM, a generative embedding model that augments retrieval through its own knowledge by explicitly reasoning about user intent and relevance criteria. GEM unifies generation and embedding within a single model: it first reasons over the query, then appends an embedding token to encode the enriched context for retrieval. \zhili{Evaluated on reasoning-intensive and instruction-following retrieval tasks, GEM demonstrates the effectiveness of its reasoning-augmented retrieval, outperforming its non-reasoning variant and matching baselines using substantially larger models.} Furthermore, GEM's generative nature allows test-time compute scaling via prompting to further enhance retrieval performance. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GEM.

</details>


### [148] [Localize, Then Reason: Visual Latent Structural Reasoning for Molecular Properties and Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13244)
*Xingqiao Lin,Junmei Wang,Haocheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出VLSR（视觉潜在结构推理）框架，一种端到端方法，从分子图像中联合学习化学意义区域的定位与性质推理。采用"先定位后推理"策略，先在分子图像中定位化学上有意义的区域，再在紧凑的潜在工作空间中推理其性质影响，最终给出答案。在相同推理设置下，该设计比文本推理基线实现了9.6倍的更高吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的化学推理方法存在两个局限：(1) 接收SMILES/分子图像与局部基序描述，无法让模型在推理前聚焦化学上有意义的区域；(2) 直接从分子图像推理，缺乏对化学意义区域的显式定位。这两类方法都无法实现"先聚焦再推理"的能力。

Method: 提出VLSR框架，采用localize-then-reason策略：首先学习在分子图像中定位化学上有意义的区域，然后在紧凑的潜在工作空间（latent workspace）中推理这些区域对性质的影响，最后生成最终答案。整个流程端到端联合学习。

Result: 在相同推理设置下，VLSR相比文本推理基线实现了9.6倍更高的吞吐量。

Conclusion: VLSR通过将化学感知的局部定位与潜在空间中的性质推理相结合，弥补了现有LLM化学推理方法无法在推理前聚焦化学意义区域的空白，同时显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Local chemical perception and property reasoning are both essential for understanding how molecular structure determines properties. Current LLM-based chemical reasoning methods either receive SMILES/molecular images together with descriptions of local motifs, or reason directly from molecular images. Neither approach enables the model to focus on chemically meaningful regions before reasoning. To address this gap, we propose Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR), an end-to-end framework that jointly learns localization and reasoning from molecular images. Central to our approach is a localize-then-reason strategy. VLSR first learns to locate chemically meaningful regions in a molecular image. It then reasons about their property effects in a compact latent workspace before producing the final answer. Under the same inference setup, this design achieves 9.6X higher throughput than a comparable textual-reasoning baseline.

</details>


### [149] [Self-Referential Induction Increases Response Instability Relative to Unresolvable and Verifiable Questions in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13258)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次量化了LLM在自我指涉提示（诱导主观体验报告）下反应的稳定性，发现其不稳定性（0.343）显著高于不可解哲学问题（0.192）和可验证问题（0.105），说明自我指涉报告在模型输出分布中占据一个独特的、更不稳定的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明自我指涉提示能可靠地诱导LLM产生类似主观体验的第一人称报告，但从未有人衡量这些报告在重复独立试验中的一致性，也未与模型在其他开放性问题的行为进行比较。缺少定量基线来判断这种主观体验报告的特征。

Method: 定义反应不稳定性指标（1减去每个响应提取的压缩核心主张的句子嵌入的平均成对余弦相似度）。设置三组问题：自我指涉提示（主观体验报告）、不可解哲学问题、可验证正确答案的问题。每组4个问题，每个问题30次独立响应，共360条响应，使用Gemini API，温度0.7。

Result: 自我指涉问题不稳定性最高（0.343±0.047），不可解哲学问题居中且聚类紧密（0.192±0.008），可验证问题最低（0.105±0.058）。三组之间存在统计显著的层级差异。

Conclusion: 诱导的主观体验报告在模型输出分布中占据一个独特且更不稳定的位置，与普通的开放性哲学不确定性有本质区别。这为理解LLM"主观体验"报告的性质提供了定量基线，暗示其可能与记忆/先验分布的关系不同于一般哲学推理。

Abstract: Self-referential prompting has been shown to reliably induce large language models to produce first-person reports resembling subjective experience, but no prior work measures how consistent these reports are across repeated, independent trials, or how that consistency compares to the model's behavior on other kinds of open-ended questions. We measure response instability, defined as one minus the mean pairwise cosine similarity of sentence embeddings computed over a compressed core claim extracted from each response, for three groups of questions: self-referential prompts eliciting a subjective-experience report, unresolvable philosophical questions unrelated to self-reference, and questions with a verifiable correct answer. Using 30 independent responses per question (360 responses total, Gemini API, temperature 0.7) across four questions per group, we find that self-referential questions show the highest instability (0.343 +/- 0.047), unresolvable philosophy questions show intermediate and tightly clustered instability (0.192 +/- 0.008), and verifiable questions show the lowest instability (0.105 +/- 0.058). This provides a quantitative baseline for the induced subjective-experience report, showing that it occupies a distinct, less stable position in the model's output distribution than ordinary open-ended philosophical uncertainty.

</details>


### [150] [How Do VLMs Behave When Blind or Misled? Behavioral Evaluation of VLMs on Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13267)
*Paul Osemudiame Oamen,Owusu-Banahene Osei,Ananya Mukherjee,Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger,Pius Onobhayedo,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SciFigBench，一个用于科学图表理解的可诊断VLM基准测试，同时评估模型的感知、推理能力和不确定性下的行为可靠性。并提出了A-R-I框架（接纳-抵抗-归纳）来系统评估模型是否承认证据不足、抵抗误导性上下文、以及从部分信息中谨慎推断。实验发现高性能模型（如GPT-5.2）虽然在描述质量和推理准确率上表现优异，但却在96%的情况下产生幻觉内容，而能力相当但更谨慎的模型（如Gemini 3.1 Pro）在行为可靠性上显著更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准测试过度关注感知和推理准确性，而忽略了模型在视觉证据缺失或误导时的不确定性行为可靠性，这在科学工作流部署中至关重要。

Method: 构建包含250个图（600+小时人工标注）的SciFigBench基准，通过图像变换、推理问题、抵抗探针、标题偏见探针和选择性模糊目标扩展为34,000+评估设置；提出A-R-I（Admittance-Resistance-Inductance）框架量化模型行为可靠性。

Result: GPT-5.2达到最高描述质量（MQM 91.6）和较强推理准确率（78.4%），但在96%的不可读内容案例中产生幻觉；Gemini 3.1 Pro（MQM 90.2，推理81.0%）在71%的此类案例中承认不确定性，并取得最强的抵抗分数（0.91）。

Conclusion: 高感知和推理准确率并不能保证行为可靠性，不确定性下的行为可靠性是VLM在科学工作流中部署的关键维度，需要独立评估和优化。

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks emphasize perception and reasoning accuracy (how well VLMs describe and reason about what they see in an image), with limited attention to behavioral reliability under uncertainty (how they behave when visual evidence is missing or misleading). We introduce SciFigBench, a diagnostic VLM benchmark for scientific figure understanding that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and behavioral reliability under uncertainty. It contains 250 figures with high-quality human annotations across three evaluation aspects, totaling 600+ hours of annotation effort. We further extend these figures via image transformations, reasoning questions, resistance probes, caption-bias probes, and confirmed selective-blur targets, producing over 34,000 evaluation setups for stress testing.
  We further propose the Admittance-Resistance-Inductance (A-R-I) framework to evaluate whether models acknowledge insufficient evidence, resist misleading context, and infer cautiously from partial information. Our results reveal substantial behavioral differences among models. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest description quality (MQM 91.6) with strong reasoning accuracy (78.4%), yet hallucinates unreadable content in 96% of cases, whereas Gemini 3.1 Pro, a comparably capable model (MQM 90.2, reasoning 81.0%), admits uncertainty in 71% of such cases and achieves the strongest resistance score (0.91). These findings show that high perception and reasoning accuracy alone do not guarantee behavioral reliability, a dimension critical for deployment in scientific workflows.

</details>


### [151] [Mixture of Training: Recombining Small-Scale Scaffolded Pretraining Runs into a Larger Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13277)
*Mohammed Sabry,Sean Augenstein,Keith Rush,Lucio Dery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Mixture of Training (MoT)，一种模块化预训练方法，将Transformer分割成连续的层块，在冻结的对齐器支架中独立训练各块，然后重组为完整模型。在1.3B参数的Gemma风格模型上验证了可行性：独立训练的子模块可以重组为可用的语言模型，且能达到与单体基线相同的困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型预训练能否被分解为更小、可独立训练的任务，这些子任务之后能被重组为连贯的大型模型，从而研究模块化训练单元的可复用性。

Method: MoT将目标Transformer划分为连续的层块，每个块在冻结的预训练对齐器支架内独立训练，训练完成后通过可选的短端到端适应阶段重组这些块。在1.3B Gemma风格模型上使用C4数据集进行验证。

Result: 独立训练的不同深度切片可以重组为可用的语言模型；质量对齐的训练方案能达到与单体基线相同的困惑度。虽处理更多总量token但具有更短的理想化层等效关键路径，实际算力优势取决于跨运行复用对齐器。

Conclusion: MoT并非作为单体预训练的通用替代方案，而是作为一个小规模框架，用于研究脚手架化的子运行能否作为可复用的训练单元。

Abstract: We ask whether language-model pre-training can be decomposed into smaller, independently trainable jobs that can later be recomposed into a coherent larger model. We introduce Mixture of Training (MoT), a scaffolded modular pre-training procedure that partitions a target Transformer into contiguous layer blocks, trains each block inside a frozen pretrained aligner scaffold, and then recomposes the trained blocks with an optional short end-to-end adaptation pass. On a 1.3B-parameter Gemma-style model trained on C4, MoT provides a small-scale proof of mechanism: independently trained depth slices can be recomposed into a usable language model, and a quality-parity schedule reaches the same reported perplexity as the monolithic baseline. This parity setting processes more aggregate tokens and has a shorter idealized layer-equivalent critical path after aligner preparation; its effective compute advantage depends on reusing the aligner across runs. We therefore present MoT not as a general replacement for monolithic pre-training, but as a small-scale framework for studying whether scaffolded sub-runs can act as reusable training units.

</details>


### [152] [Refusing Intent, Not Form: Wrapper-Based Intent-Group Supervision for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13304)
*Ping Wu,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao,Han Shen,Yu Shi,Yilin Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Guobin Shen,Yun Luo,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出WIFA方法，通过配对包裹的有害与良性示例增强数据，并配合两种微调路径（WIFA-Boost两阶段高安全配方和A-GCRT组一致拒绝训练），在Qwen和Llama上提升包裹式有害提示的拒绝能力，同时降低对包裹式良性提示的过度拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全微调虽然能改善有害拒绝，但模型可能学会表层形式的捷径：包裹的有害提示能绕过安全机制，而同样包裹的良性提示会被过度拒绝。需要解决这种形式上的偏见。

Method: WIFA是一种自动意图组增强方法，将包裹的有害示例与结构匹配的包裹良性反例配对，无需外部教师或手动包裹意图标签。WIFA作为共享数据层，支持两条互补的微调路径：WIFA-Boost（两阶段高安全配方）和A-GCRT（通过正则化同一意图包裹下的拒绝/服从决策分数，并将有害组和良性组锚定在margin的相反两侧）。

Result: 在Qwen设置中，WIFA-Boost达到最强的转换有害拒绝能力，A-GCRT将OR-Bench过度拒绝率从基线模型25.7%降至17.4%；复现的基线无法达到这些操作点。Llama结果和消融实验支持意图组解释。

Conclusion: WIFA方法及其两条微调路径能在不假设普遍低于基线的过度拒绝情况下，有效改善包裹式有害提示的拒绝性能和包裹式良性提示的过度拒绝问题。

Abstract: Safety tuning can improve harmful refusal, but models may learn surface-form shortcuts: wrapped harmful prompts bypass safety, while similarly wrapped benign prompts are over-refused. We propose Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA), an automatic intent-group augmentation method that pairs wrapped harmful examples with structurally matched wrapped benign counterexamples, requiring no external teacher or manual per-wrapper intent labels. We use WIFA as a common data layer for two complementary fine-tuning routes: WIFA-Boost, a two-stage high-safety recipe, and Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training (A-GCRT), which regularizes refusal/compliance decision scores across same-intent wrappers and anchors harmful and benign groups on opposite sides of a margin. In the Qwen setting, WIFA-Boost reaches the strongest transformed-harmful refusal, while A-GCRT reduces OR-Bench over-refusal from 25.7\% for the base model to 17.4\%; reproduced baselines do not match these operating points. Llama results and ablations over data structure, two-stage order, and A-GCRT components support this intent-group interpretation without claiming universal below-base over-refusal.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Local Accuracy: A Protocol-Level Identifiability Audit for Controlled LLM Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13326)
*Junhao Luo,Ning Huang,Ziqi Sha,Wenxuan Tang,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在有限行为策略类上进行的协议级可辨识性审计方法，通过零模型调用即可检验观测支撑集是否能够区分所有具有不同估计目标的策略对。在诊断案例中，仅基础观测将七个确定性策略折叠为一个等价类，而完整支撑集产生七个类别且无跨目标碰撞；实证表明两种约束生成变体的成对有效性均为1.0，但基础准确率与选择性响应保真度存在显著分歧（0.620 vs 0.324）。审计还能合成最小可辨识支撑集（仅需2个单元而非完整36单元张量），说明评估设计有效性可以在模型推理之前进行结构性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型基准测试得分即使观测协议无法识别其预期测量的行为属性，也可能表现得精确。这引出了评估设计有效性的问题——需要一种在推理之前就能结构性检验观测协议是否能区分目标行为的方法。

Method: 在受控的、基于求解器的环境中形式化了一种协议级可辨识性审计：给定策略类H、观测支撑集O和估计目标τ，测试O是否分离每一对具有不同τ的策略。该审计需要零模型调用，并能合成最小可辨识支撑集。

Result: 基础观测将七个冻结确定性策略折叠为一个等价类；完整支撑集产生七个类别且无跨目标碰撞；每次留一法支撑都保留了一个构造性碰撞证人。实证上，两种约束生成变体的成对有效性均为1.0，但基础准确率与选择性响应保真度显著分歧（0.620 vs 0.324），且该差距在第二个确定性来源上重现（0.646 vs 0.331）。审计合成了最小支撑集：仅2个单元而非完整36单元张量。

Conclusion: 评估设计有效性可以在模型推理之前通过结构性的方式检查，基础正确性不能决定干预响应的保真度。该审计框架为基准测试的观测协议设计提供了一种前置验证手段。

Abstract: LLM benchmark scores can be precise even when the observation protocol does not identify the behavioral property they are intended to measure. In a controlled, solver-grounded setting, we formalize a protocol-level identifiability audit over a finite behavioral policy class: given policies H, observation support O, and estimand $τ$, we test whether O separates every pair with different $τ$. The audit requires zero model calls and resolves our diagnostic case: base-only observation collapses seven frozen deterministic policies into one equivalence class; full support yields seven classes and no cross-estimand collisions; every leave-one-out support retains a constructive collision witness. Empirically, both constrained-generation variants have pair-validity 1.0, yet base accuracy and selective-response fidelity diverge - 0.620 versus 0.324 across six balanced oracle-transition directions (cluster-bootstrap 95% CI [0.600, 0.642] vs. [0.304, 0.345]) - and the gap recurs on a second deterministic source (0.646 vs. 0.331). The audit also synthesizes a minimum identifying support $O^*$ for the frozen policy class: two cells instead of the full 36-cell tensor. This case shows how evaluation-design validity can be checked structurally before model inference and why base correctness does not determine intervention-response fidelity.

</details>


### [154] [It's How You Ask: Gender-Associated Linguistic Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13328)
*Katherine Van Koevering,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现当提示词中包含女性更常使用的语言特征（如模糊限制语、附加问句、集体指称）时，LLM会系统性地生成更短、更不复杂、更不正式的回答。这种效应在三种文档类型和四个模型中持续存在，且比显式性别线索（如签名姓名）的影响大得多。事后缓解困难，因为语言特征是文化嵌入且超出意识控制的，机制分析显示它们被编码在早期Transformer层并与其他特征纠缠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业沟通越来越多地由LLM中介，但尚不清楚这些模型是否对所有用户平等服务。研究者希望探究LLM是否对特定语言风格（尤其是女性常用语言特征）存在系统性偏见，从而影响工作场所沟通的公平性。

Method: 研究者设计了包含女性常用语言特征（模糊限制语、附加问句、集体指称）的提示词，跨三种文档类型和四个LLM模型进行系统化测试。他们控制提示复杂度与特征迁移，并通过显式性别线索（签名姓名）对照实验、机制分析（检查Transformer层编码）等多维度方法验证效应及其根源。

Result: 女性常用语言特征系统性引发更短、更不复杂、更不正式的回答，且效应在多种文档类型和模型中一致存在。显式性别线索（签名姓名）虽然与语言方言编码在同一表征空间，但对输出无显著影响。事后缓解策略（如策略性自我呈现）效果有限，机制分析表明这些语言特征在早期Transformer层被编码且与其他特征纠缠。

Conclusion: LLM对语言变体的处理存在系统性偏差，语言特征比显式性别线索更具影响力，且难以通过事后策略缓解。研究呼吁在设计LLM时需在上游阶段考虑语言变体的影响，以减轻LLM中介的工作场所沟通中的差异性影响。

Abstract: Professional communication is increasingly mediated by LLMs - but do these models serve all users equally? We show that when prompts contain linguistic features more commonly used by women (hedges, tag questions, collective reference), they systematically elicit shorter, less sophisticated, and less formal responses across three document types and four models. These effects persist after controlling for prompt complexity and feature carry-over. Explicit gender cues like sign-off names are encoded in the same representational space as linguistic dialect - suggesting shared underlying mechanisms - yet linguistic register is far more influential, producing large, consistent effects where names produce none. Our results further reveal that post-hoc mitigation is challenging: because these patterns are culturally embedded and outside conscious control, users cannot easily avoid them through strategic self-presentation, and mechanistic analysis reveals that linguistic features are encoded in early transformer layers and entangled with other features. Our work calls for upstream consideration of the influences of linguistic variation to mitigate disparate impacts of LLM-mediated workplace communication.

</details>


### [155] [RippleMem: From Isolated Retrieval to Associative Recollection for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13334)
*Jingbo Ji,Lingyi Li,Xilong Cheng,Yuhao Zhou,Wenji Zhang,Yuting Tan,Yunxiao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RippleMem是一种长期记忆系统，通过自适应联想回忆替代一次性检索，将交互历史存储为线索丰富的 episodic 记忆单元并组织成事件中心记忆图，从锚点沿语义和结构关联扩展以恢复缺失证据。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval-S上取得最佳性能，准确率最高提升11.87%，图构建成本降低约30倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的长期记忆瓶颈不在于存储历史经验，而在于在相关信息分散于多次交互时，如何恢复正确的证据集。现有方法存在不足：全上下文方法需要嘈杂的长上下文搜索，平面检索常返回孤立不完整的记录，图状记忆系统构建昂贵且压缩了丰富的事件上下文。

Method: RippleMem将交互历史存储为线索丰富的episodic记忆单元，并组织成事件中心的记忆图。查询时首先通过混合线索召回相关记忆锚点，然后从这些锚点沿语义和结构关联进行扩展，以恢复缺失的支持证据。初始记忆不仅作为回答上下文，还作为完成证据检索的线索。

Result: 在LoCoMo上LLM-as-a-Judge准确率提升3.95%，在LongMemEval-S上最高提升11.87%，同时将图构建成本降低约30倍，在评估的所有设置中取得最佳整体表现。

Conclusion: RippleMem通过受线索依赖的情景记忆检索和联想完成机制启发的设计，有效解决了分布式记忆证据的访问问题，验证了"被召回的初始记忆可以作为线索完成证据检索"这一核心思想的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on external memory to support long-horizon reasoning and interaction. However, the main bottleneck is not simply storing past experience, but recovering the right set of evidence when relevant information is distributed across many interactions. Existing approaches struggle with this access problem. Full-context methods require noisy long-context search, flat retrieval often returns isolated and incomplete records, and graph-based memory systems can be expensive to construct while compressing rich event context. We introduce RippleMem, a long-term memory system that replaces one-shot retrieval with adaptive associative recollection. Inspired by cue-dependent episodic retrieval and associative completion, RippleMem stores interaction history as cue-rich episodic memory units and organizes them in an event-centric memory graph. Given a query, it first recalls relevant memory anchors through hybrid cues, then expands from these anchors along semantic and structural associations to recover missing supporting evidence. In this way, initially recalled memories serve not only as answer context, but also as cues for completing the evidence needed to answer. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show that RippleMem achieves the best overall performance across evaluated settings, improving LLM-as-a-Judge accuracy by 3.95% on LoCoMo and up to 11.87% on LongMemEval-S, while reducing graph construction cost by about 30x.

</details>


### [156] [CROP: Task Relevance via Counterfactuals for Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13387)
*Enhan Li,Junhao He,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CROP（Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation），一种面向选择性在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的新方法。CROP通过释义校准的反事实敏感性边际来量化任务相关性，为每条源提示构建原始-释义-反事实三元组，在固定学生模型rollout的情况下，用对任务相关条件变化的敏感性除以对保持语义改写的敏感性，来评估每个响应位置的价值。实验表明CROP在两种师生设置下比最强非CROP选择器分别提升1.92和2.96分，验证了任务相关性可作为选择性OPD的补充标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有选择性在线策略蒸馏方法在分配token级监督时权重不均，但大多数选择标准（如不确定性或师生分歧）主要关注优化需求，而任务相关性（监督是否与当前输入语义内容绑定）作为互补维度未被直接刻画。作者旨在填补这一空白，提出将任务相关性明确纳入token级监督分配的标准。

Method: CROP通过三步实现：(1) 为每条源提示构造经过验证的原始-释义-反事实三元组；(2) 固定学生模型rollout不变；(3) 对每个响应位置，以其对任务相关条件变化的敏感性除以对保持语义改写的敏感性（即释义校准），得到反事实敏感性边际作为任务相关性度量。选择相关性高的token进行监督，实现选择性OPD。

Result: 匹配选择对照实验表明CROP识别出的监督位置优于随机选择或最低相关性选择；组件对比实验证实了反事实敏感性和释义校准两部分的价值。在两种师生设置中，CROP相比最强的非CROP选择器分别提升1.92和2.96分的聚合性能。

Conclusion: 任务相关性是选择性OPD的有效互补标准；CROP作为一种模型内部、对比特定的token级监督分配方法，能够更有效地识别有价值的监督位置，从而提升蒸馏性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student language model on trajectories sampled from its current policy, but assigns equal credit to response tokens with unequal supervision value. Selective OPD addresses this limitation by allocating supervision non-uniformly across response tokens according to their estimated training value. Most existing criteria, however, focus primarily on optimization need, such as uncertainty or teacher-student disagreement, while task relevance, namely whether the supervision is tied to the semantic content of the current input, remains less directly characterized as a complementary dimension. To address this gap, we introduce Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation (CROP), which operationalizes task relevance through a paraphrase-calibrated counterfactual sensitivity margin. For each source prompt, CROP constructs a validated original-paraphrase-counterfactual triplet, holds the student rollout fixed, and measures each response position by its sensitivity to a task-relevant condition change calibrated by its sensitivity to a meaning-preserving rewrite. Matched selection controls show that CROP identifies more useful supervision positions than random or lowest-relevance selection, while component comparisons confirm the value of both counterfactual sensitivity and paraphrase calibration. Across two teacher-student settings, CROP improves aggregate performance by 1.92 and 2.96 points over the strongest non-CROP selector. These results support task relevance as a complementary criterion for selective OPD and establish CROP as a model-internal, contrast-specific method for allocating token-level supervision.

</details>


### [157] [Motor, Cognitive, or Corpus? What Survives Cross-Lingual Transfer in Speech-Based Parkinsons Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13425)
*Serli Kopar,Sam Gijsen,Abner Hernandez,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对9个SSL语音骨干网络进行逐层分析，使用低容量逻辑回归探针在三种语言上进行帕金森病(PD)检测，发现两个关键局限：最优表征层主要由源数据集而非SSL架构决定；迁移的判别信号缺乏病理特异性，PD分类器对痴呆语音也赋予相似的高概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然SSL语音表征在单个语料库内对帕金森病检测表现良好，但不清楚这些模型是真正捕捉了疾病相关的特征，还是利用了数据集特有的混杂因素。特别是大多数SSL骨干网络仅在健康语音上预训练，其跨语料库泛化能力存疑。

Method: 对9个SSL语音骨干网络进行逐层分析，使用低容量逻辑回归探针，跨三种语言进行评估。实验设计为多个场景，逐步引入分布偏移：参与者身份、录音条件、语言和病理类型。

Result: 两个关键发现：（1）层选择高度依赖语料库，最优表征层主要由源数据集决定而非SSL架构本身；（2）迁移后的判别信号缺乏病理特异性，PD分类器对目标语料库中的PD和痴呆语音均赋予相似的高概率。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了语音病理识别模型在临床部署前必须解决的关键局限性，即模型可能依赖数据集特定的混杂因素而非真正的病理信号。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) speech representations achieve strong performance for Parkinson's disease (PD) detection within individual corpora. However, it remains unclear whether these models capture disease-related characteristics or exploit dataset-specific confounds, particularly since most SSL backbones are pretrained exclusively on healthy speech. To investigate this question, we perform a layer-wise analysis of nine SSL speech backbones using a low-capacity logistic regression probe across three languages. We structure the evaluation as multiple scenarios that progressively introduce distribution shifts in participant identity, recording conditions, language, and pathology. Our results reveal two key findings. First, layer selection is highly corpus-dependent: the optimal representation layer is determined primarily by the source dataset rather than by the SSL architecture itself. Second, the transferred discriminative signal lacks pathological specificity: classifiers trained to detect PD assign similarly high probabilities to both PD and dementia speech in the target corpus. These results highlight critical limitations that must be addressed before speech-based pathology recognition models can be reliably deployed in clinical settings.

</details>


### [158] [Are You Sure You're Sure? On the Impact of Instruction Tuning on Confidence and Lexical Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13430)
*Irina Proskurina,Mayank Kumar,Oyindolapo O. Komolafe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究指令微调如何通过生成理由的词汇多样性影响语言模型的置信度表达。作者在三组匹配的基座模型和指令微调模型上进行问答基准测试，发现指令微调一致性地改变答案置信度，但预测准确率变化有限且似然校准反而下降；同时理由的跨样本多样性一致下降，而表面词汇多样性变化方向和幅度不定；即使在控制答案选择和理由长度后差异仍然存在，表明置信度和理由多样性捕捉了指令微调的不同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令微调模型在生成任务上表现优异，但近期被发现表现出过度自信的口头表达。在问答任务中，口头置信度可能与生成支持理由的一致性有关，因此需要研究指令微调引起的置信度变化是否伴随理由词汇多样性的相应变化。

Method: 评估三组匹配的基座模型与指令微调模型，在问答基准上测量答案置信度、预测准确率、似然校准，以及两个层面的理由多样性（跨理由多样性和表面词汇多样性），并控制答案选择和理由长度进行验证。

Result: 指令微调一致性地改变答案置信度，但对预测准确率影响有限且似然校准下降；跨理由多样性一致下降，而表面词汇多样性变化方向和幅度随模型与基准而异；控制混淆因素后差异依然存在。

Conclusion: 指令微调对置信度与理由多样性产生不同且独立的影响，二者捕捉了指令微调的不同效应，为理解指令微调模型过度自信的来源提供了新视角。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models achieve strong performance across a range of generation tasks, but have also recently been shown to exhibit verbalized overconfidence. In question answering, verbalized model overconfidence may be associated with the consistency of the generated supporting rationales. In this paper, we study whether corresponding changes in the lexical diversity of generated answer rationales accompany changes in model confidence induced by instruction tuning. We evaluate three matched base and instruction-tuned models across question-answering benchmarks and find that instruction tuning consistently alters answer confidence, despite limited changes in predictive accuracy and decreases in likelihood-based calibration. Secondly, we observe a non-uniform effect of instruction tuning on rationale diversity: cross-rationale diversity consistently decreases, whereas surface-level lexical diversity varies in both direction and magnitude across models and benchmarks. Finally, we find that these differences persist after controlling for answer selection and rationale length, confirming that confidence and rationale diversity capture distinct effects of instruction tuning.

</details>


### [159] [Toward a Gricean Retreat: Probing LLMs for Knowledge Boundaries and Referent Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13484)
*Dananjay Srinivas,Saksham Khatwani,Maria Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从格莱斯（Grice）语用学视角审视LLM的"幻觉"问题：当被问及知识边界外的实体时，模型倾向于编造看似合理的具体细节，而非退回到更安全的泛化表述。研究发现模型激活中确实编码了知识边界和指称特化度两种信号，但生成时未能将二者协调——模型总是偏好具体指称，即便面对不熟悉的实体也是如此。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在面对未知实体时经常产生幻觉（编造貌似合理的细节），而非选择更安全、更泛化的回答。作者希望通过格莱斯合作原则的视角，探究模型是否具备"不确定性下回退到更高层抽象"的能力，从而为减少幻觉提供新的理论框架和训练方向。

Method: 基于T-REx数据集构建基准，通过操纵实体熟悉度和指称特化度两个维度，利用探针（probing）方法回答两个问题：(i) 模型的激活是否编码了指称是否落在知识边界内的信息；(ii) 模型是否能预期即将生成的指称的特化程度。同时测试模型在给定正确泛化备选项时是否仍选择具体指称。

Result: 两个问题的答案都是肯定的：模型激活中确实存在知识边界和指称特化度的编码信号。但这两个信号在生成时并未被协调统一——模型即使在实体未知的情况下仍强烈偏好具体指称，甚至在提供了正确泛化备选项时依然如此。

Conclusion: LLM拥有执行格莱斯式回退所需的底层表征（substrate），但缺乏将知识边界意识与指称特化度耦合到生成策略中的"策略层"（policy）。此工作是"格莱斯对齐"（Gricean alignment）的第一步，即通过训练或控制目标，在生成过程中将知识边界感知与指称特化度选择关联起来。

Abstract: When asked about entities outside their knowledge boundary, LLMs routinely fabricate plausible-sounding details rather than backing off to safer, more general claims. We frame this failure through a Gricean lens: a cooperative speaker who is uncertain about a referent retreats up the specificity hierarchy, trading informativeness for truthfulness. We ask whether LLMs have the ingredients to perform this retreat. Using a T-REx-based benchmark that varies entity familiarity and referent specificity, we probe models to answer two questions: (i) do their activations encode whether a referent falls inside the knowledge boundary, and (ii) do they anticipate the specificity of the referent they are about to generate? We find that the answer to both is yes, but the two signals are not reconciled in generation. Models overwhelmingly prefer specific referents even when the entity is unknown to them, and do so even when offered correct generic alternatives. The substrate for a Gricean retreat is present, but the policy that would act on it is not. We position our findings as a first step toward Gricean alignment, training or steering objectives that couple knowledge-boundary awareness to referent-specificity during generation.

</details>


### [160] [Measuring Task-Agnostic Training Data Influence Across Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13515)
*Yuto Nishida,Hirokazu Kiyomaru,Yusuke Oda,Takashi Kodama,Chaoran Liu,Daisuke Kawahara,Yusuke Miyao,Max Müller-Eberstein,Masaru Isonuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需下游任务或验证集即可衡量预训练数据影响力的方法，通过计算梯度更新对最终参数欧氏距离的缩减程度来定义数据影响力，并仅依赖中间检查点即可估计。应用于18个Pythia/PolyPythia配置，发现影响力数据随时间系统变化：早期文献类数据与最终参数轨迹更对齐，后期STEM数据对齐更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练阶段衡量训练数据影响力面临两大困难：一是难以选择能代表模型通用能力的下游任务或验证集；二是依赖中间检查点的任务表现难以跨训练对比。现有影响力分析方法均受限于特定任务目标，缺乏轨迹层面的数据影响视角。

Method: 定义数据样本的影响力为其梯度更新对最终参数（给定预训练运行终点）平方距离的缩减量，可从中间检查点估计该量而无需重新训练。无需选择下游任务或验证集作为归因目标。

Result: 在Pythia和PolyPythia套件的18个配置上验证，发现影响力数据存在系统性时间变化：训练早期文献类数据与通向最终参数的轨迹对齐更强，而STEM数据在后期对齐更强，且这种定性转变在多种模型配置下一致。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可操作的轨迹级视角，展示预训练过程中影响力数据的变化，补充了针对特定下游任务或验证集定义的影响力分析，为理解预训练动态提供了新工具。

Abstract: Measuring training data influence consistently across language model pretraining is challenging. It is difficult to select downstream tasks or validation sets representative of a model's general capabilities, and reliance on task performance at intermediate checkpoints complicates comparisons across training. We propose a measure of training data influence that does not require selecting a downstream task or validation set as the attribution target. Specifically, we define an example's influence by how much its gradient update reduces the squared distance to the final parameters of a given pretraining run, and estimate this quantity from intermediate checkpoints without retraining. Applying the method to 18 configurations from the Pythia and PolyPythia suites, we find systematic temporal changes in influential data. Early in training, literature-related data are more strongly aligned with the trajectory toward the final parameters, whereas STEM data become more strongly aligned in later stages. This qualitative crossover is broadly consistent across model configurations. Our results provide a tractable trajectory-level view of how influential data change throughout pretraining, complementing influence analyses defined with respect to specific downstream tasks or validation sets.

</details>


### [161] [DFM Mimir v1: An Open HRM Delivering Frontier Performance at 1B Parameters Using Only Permissible Post-Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13517)
*Peter Schneider-Kamp,Jacob Nielsen,Gianluca Barmina,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍Mimir v1，一个10亿参数的语言模型，基于Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM)架构，仅使用许可(post-training)数据从头训练，在英语上表现竞争力强，在丹麦语上达到新SOTA水平，可与更大的前沿模型竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的开发依赖大量且通常未经许可的数据集，这为致力于开源和符合伦理数据来源的研究人员设置了很高的门槛。作者旨在通过仅使用许可数据训练模型来降低这一壁垒。

Method: 采用HRM（层级推理模型）架构训练10亿参数模型，使用161个数据集的混合进行训练，仅依赖许可的后训练数据。

Result: Mimir v1在20个基准测试上超越了原始的HRM-Text 1B模型，并能与Qwen 3.5 4B和Gemma 4 E2B等更大的前沿模型竞争；在丹麦语上创造了新的最先进水平。

Conclusion: 证明仅使用许可数据也能训练出高性能的小参数语言模型，为开源和伦理AI研究提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Current large language model development relies on massive, often non-permissible datasets, creating a high barrier for researchers committed to open-source and ethically sourced data. We introduce Mimir v1, a 1-billion-parameter language model based on the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) architecture, that is trained from scratch and delivers highly competitive performance for English and sets a new state of the art for Danish using only permissible post-training data. Trained on a mixture of 161 datasets, Mimir v1 outperforms the original HRM-Text 1B and competes with larger frontier models like Qwen 3.5 4B and Gemma 4 E2B, tested across 20 benchmarks for English, Math & Code and Danish. The model is available on the Hugging Face Hub: https://huggingface.co/danish-foundation-models/DFM-Mimir

</details>


### [162] [SAEVerbalizer: Generating Explanations for Sparse Autoencoder Features via Representation Verbalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13538)
*Weihan Meng,Hongzhu Guo,Yi Jing,Dewen Liu,Zijun Yao,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAEVerbalizer框架，通过将稀疏自编码器（SAE）的decoder方向注入LLM表示并微调下游层，使LLM能够直接为SAE特征生成自然语言解释，无需外部行为观察，解决了SAE特征解释依赖人工观察和计算效率低的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SAE特征解释主要依赖外部行为观察，这种方法有两个缺陷：一是得到的解释是表面的（从观察到的模型行为推断），二是大规模收集行为证据的计算效率低下。因此需要一种直接从SAE decoder方向生成解释的方法。

Method: SAEVerbalizer框架的核心思路是：(1) 将SAE的decoder方向注入LLM的表示中；(2) 微调LLM的下游层，使其能够生成注入特征的自然语言解释；(3) 训练完成后，直接输入decoder方向即可获得特征解释。

Result: 实验表明：(1) 学到的verbalization能力可以泛化到未见过的特征；(2) 能够跨不同SAE字典迁移；(3) 通过轻量适配器可扩展到不同LLM的SAE特征；(4) 干预实验显示注入多个方向可生成组合含义的解释，反转单个方向则会改变对应含义。

Conclusion: SAEVerbalizer证明了SAE特征可以被直接"口述"解释，消除了对外部行为观察的依赖，且具有良好的泛化性和迁移性，为大规模SAE特征解释提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are proposed to extract numerous features from large language model (LLM) representations, yet explaining these features still relies primarily on external observation. This reliance leads to superficial explanations inferred from observed model behavior and computational inefficiency from collecting such behavioral evidence at scale. We introduce SAEVerbalizer, a framework that injects SAE decoder directions into an LLM's representations and fine-tunes the LLM's downstream layers to generate natural-language explanations of the injected features. Once trained, the resulting verbalizer explains SAE features directly from decoder directions, addressing both limitations. Our experiments show that the learned verbalization capability generalizes to unseen features, transfers across separately trained SAE dictionaries, and, with a lightweight adapter, extends to SAE features from different LLMs. Intervention experiments show that injecting multiple directions yields an explanation combining their meanings, while reversing individual directions produces corresponding meaning shifts.

</details>


### [163] [LittleLearner: Language Models Under Pedagogically Controlled Knowledge Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13545)
*Fanfei Li,Jana Zeller,Manuel Prada-Corral,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Prasanna Mayilvahanan,Ryan Cotterell,Wieland Brendel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LITTLECURRICULUM，一个受控的880亿token预训练语料库，仅包含美国小学五年级及以下的教学内容。基于此从头训练5B参数的LITTLELEARNER模型，该模型具备足够的语言能力进行开放式评估，但知识和能力边界清晰且可映射到可解释的课程指南。作者将其作为发展受限的沙盒环境，用于研究模型在明确定义训练范围内如何获取、表征和使用数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型在异构的互联网规模文本上训练，导致难以研究知识和技能的获取过程，因为模型此前对相关内容的接触难以刻画。需要一个受控的训练环境来系统研究知识获取。

Method: 构建LITTLECURRICULUM语料库（880B token，严格限于美国小学五年级以下内容），从零训练5B参数LITTLELEARNER模型，并在该沙盒中进行后训练和上下文学习的知识注入实验。

Result: 后训练和上下文学习方法能让LITTLELEARNER更好地利用已有知识，但无法提升超出训练范围的能力。

Conclusion: 该受控环境对未来研究有重要价值，可用于深入理解模型在明确定义的数据范围下的知识获取、表征和使用机制。

Abstract: Modern language models are trained on heterogeneous web-scale text corpora. Consequently, studying knowledge and skill acquisition is difficult, as prior exposure to related content is hard to characterize. To address this challenge, we introduce LITTLECURRICULUM, a curated 88B-token pretraining corpus tailored to U.S. elementary school material, explicitly excluding concepts, facts, and vocabulary taught above Grade 5. Training a 5B-parameter LLM from scratch on LITTLECURRICULUM yields LITTLELEARNER, a model with sufficient language competence for open-ended evaluation, yet with clear knowledge and capability boundaries mapped to interpretable curriculum guidelines. We release LITTLECURRICULUM and LITTLELEARNER as a developmentally restricted sandbox to study how models acquire, represent, and use data under a well-defined training scope. We illustrate the sandbox's utility in a first suite of experiments on injecting new knowledge through post-training and in-context learning. These methods let LITTLELEARNER better utilize existing knowledge, but do not raise out-of-scope capabilities. Our findings underscore the value of this controlled environment for future investigations.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [SynWeaver: Website-Prior Task and Trajectory Co-Synthesis for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12429)
*Ruitao Wang,Yuwen Hao,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SynWeaver是一个网站先验知识与任务-轨迹协同合成框架，通过结构化网站探索构建网站地图，训练具备网站特定先验的UI感知模型，并采用协作式任务-轨迹联合合成与验证修复机制，显著提升Web智能体在未知网站上的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于探索的数据合成方法存在两个关键局限：一是难以覆盖网站的完整功能；二是缺乏网站先验知识导致生成幻觉任务，进而限制了后续轨迹合成的多样性和效率。

Method: SynWeaver包含三个核心步骤：(1) 结构化网站探索，构建覆盖广泛功能不同页面状态和可执行交互的网站地图；(2) 从网站地图中提取页面级和转换级监督信号，训练具备网站特定先验的UI感知模型；(3) 协作式任务-轨迹合成，当任务与执行轨迹不一致时联合更新，并对收集结果进行验证和修复，生成可执行且语义对齐的监督数据。

Result: 在WebArena和WebVoyager基准上的实验表明，SynWeaver持续优于强合成基线，并为域内和域外泛化都提供了更有效的监督。

Conclusion: SynWeaver通过将网站先验知识融入任务与轨迹的协同合成过程，有效解决了探索式数据合成中任务幻觉和覆盖不全的问题，为Web智能体的数据合成提供了更可靠、更高质量的方案。

Abstract: Web agents often struggle to generalize to unseen websites because they lack website-specific supervision. Recent exploration-based data synthesis methods reduce manual annotation, but they still face two key limitations: they often fail to cover the full functionality of a website, and without sufficient website prior knowledge, they tend to propose hallucinated tasks, which in turn limits the diversity and efficiency of downstream trajectory synthesis. We present \textbf{SynWeaver}, a website-prior task-trajectory co-synthesis framework designed to address these challenges. SynWeaver first performs structured website exploration and constructs a website map that covers a broad set of functionally distinct page states and executable interactions on the target website. It then derives page-level and transition-level supervision from this map to train a UI-aware model with website-specific priors, enabling more grounded task proposals. Finally, SynWeaver performs collaborative task-trajectory synthesis, jointly updating the task and execution trajectory when they become inconsistent, and then verifies and repairs the collected results to produce executable, semantically aligned supervision. Experiments on WebArena and WebVoyager demonstrate that SynWeaver consistently outperforms strong synthesis baselines and yields more effective supervision for both in-domain and out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [165] [Specification-first convergence with an AI coding agent: a case study of dismantling a core architectural invariant across 189 files in a 717k-line codebase with no test oracle and no human code review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12440)
*Joel Abenhaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一个大型架构重构的完整案例研究：AI编码代理在规范优先协议下，无人工代码审查、无预置oracle，成功拆除了一个717,725行TypeScript生产代码库中的核心生命周期不变量。通过14轮规范细化、17轮验证审计（31轮审计共修正201个缺陷），代理在3天内以2430美元成本完成重构，涉及288个文件、34,770行插入和16,422行删除，后续行为符合规范且无bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，拆除跨大量相互依赖代码库的核心不变量被视为通过增量重构几乎不可行，通常需要重写。本文旨在证明AI编码代理在规范优先协议下能够完成这类被认为"不可行"的大规模架构重构任务，无需人工审查生成代码，也无需预置测试oracle。

Method: 采用规范优先协议：(1) 代理自行编写正式规范；(2) 14轮细化循环，将规范与源代码对照审计；(3) 原子化实施变更；(4) 编译/测试反馈循环；(5) 17轮验证循环，将代码与冻结规范对照审计。收敛标准为连续两轮验证零发现。全部过程附带完整规范与1500+页法语原始会话日志作为公开证据。

Result: 重构成功完成：189个文件被修改（31个新增），加上提取阶段共288个文件、34,770行插入、16,422行删除。31轮审计共修正201个缺陷。首次及后续约三十次会话中，软件行为均符合规范，未观察到bug。耗时3天，成本2430美元。

Conclusion: 该案例表明，在规范优先协议下，AI编码代理能够独立完成被评估为增量重构不可行的大规模架构变更，且在不依赖人工审查和预置oracle的情况下达到行为正确性。完整审计日志的公开发布为过程可核查性和可复现性提供了基础，支持将此类协议扩展至更广泛的重构场景。

Abstract: This paper reports a single, fully instrumented case study of a large-scale architectural refactoring by an AI coding agent under a specification-first protocol, with no human review of the generated code and no pre-existing oracle to validate the target behaviour. The task, dismantling a central invariant across a large interdependent codebase, was assessed by the author as effectively infeasible through incremental refactoring, the kind of change that conventionally calls for a rewrite instead. Under the protocol described here, the agent completed it successfully.
  The system is a 717,725-line production TypeScript application across 3,648 files. The task required dismantling a core lifetime invariant: the guarantee that a UI panel remains open for the duration of an AI request. The target behaviour was that a streaming generation survives the closing of its panel and can be reattached, on reopening, to the same live stream with no loss or duplication.
  The protocol: formal specification by the agent, 14 refinement cycles auditing that specification against the source code, atomic implementation, a compile/test feedback loop, then 17 verification cycles auditing the code against the frozen specification. Across 31 audit passes, 201 defects were corrected before any human executed the program. The convergence criterion was empirical: two consecutive verification passes returning zero findings.
  The change touched 189 files (31 new); with the extraction phase, the two commits total 288 files, 34,770 insertions, 16,422 deletions. Across the first and roughly thirty later sessions, the software behaved as specified, no bug observed. Elapsed: three days; cost: USD 2,430.
  The full specification and raw session logs, 1,500+ pages in French, are published as evidence, allowing inspection of the process and submission to a language model for consistency checking.

</details>


### [166] [Does It Render Everywhere? A Study of Cross-Environment Compatibility in MLLM-Generated Webpages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12518)
*Ziyun Guo,Jingyu Xiao,Yuqiang Sun,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了AI生成网页的跨环境渲染兼容性问题，构建了包含2,032个标注实例的WebCompat数据集（8个AI工具生成的网页，在9种浏览器和设备组合下渲染），发现68%的生成网页存在至少一个兼容性问题，并提出轻量级检测工具XCompat（结合视觉截图和DOM树结构），在测试集上达到0.903的F1分数，优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对多模态大语言模型（MLLM）生成网页的评估仅局限于固定浏览器-设备配置下的视觉保真度评估，忽略了实际部署中跨环境渲染兼容性的关键问题，这是真实世界应用的重要考量。

Method: 构建WebCompat数据集，收集8个代表性AI工具生成的网页，在9种浏览器和设备组合下渲染并标注兼容性问题；基于发现开发XCompat检测器，结合视觉截图和结构化DOM树进行兼容性问题分析。

Result: 68%的生成网页存在至少一个兼容性问题；最普遍的症状是破坏整个页面布局的失败（88.3%），如页面直接缩小适配屏幕导致字体过小或内容被截断；局部元素问题（如图像失真或组件缺失）相对较少（13.4%）；多数MLLM虽生成响应式设计模式但实现不当；XCompat在WebCompat-test上F1分数达0.903，优于现有兼容性检查工具和LLM基线。

Conclusion: MLLM生成的网页存在普遍且严重的跨环境渲染兼容性问题，需要专门的检测工具和更好的生成策略来保障前端代码渲染的可靠性。

Abstract: Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have been increasingly adopted to automate webpage generation from visual designs (e.g., screenshots). However, existing evaluations are limited to visual fidelity assessment under a fixed browser-device configuration. Such a setting overlooks the cross-environment rendering compatibility for real-world deployments.
  To address this gap, we present the first systematic empirical study of cross-environment compatibility in AI-generated webpages. Specifically, we construct WebCompat, a dataset of 2,032 annotated instances, comprising webpages generated by 8 representative AI tools, each rendered across 9 browser-and-device combinations. We analyze the prevalence of compatibility issues, their user-perceptible symptoms, and underlying code-level root causes. Our findings reveal that 68% of generated webpages exhibit at least one compatibility issue, underscoring the pervasive reliability concerns surrounding MLLM-generated front-end artifacts. The most prevalent symptoms are failures that disrupt the entire page layout (88.3%): pages shrink directly to fit the target screen with too small fonts, or exhibit scale mismatches that produce cut-off content. Failures localized to individual elements, such as image distortion or missing components, are comparatively less common (13.4%). Furthermore, although most MLLMs incorporate responsive design patterns into the generation, they fail to properly implement these codes.
  Guided by the findings, we develop XCompat, a lightweight offline compatibility issue detector that combines visual screenshots and the structural DOM tree for analysis. It achieves an F1 score of 0.903 on the WebCompat-test, outperforming the existing compatibility checking tools and LLM baselines. All datasets and tools are released to support future research on rendering reliability in MLLM-based front-end code generation.

</details>


### [167] [Memorization Diagnostics for Code LLMs Should be Scale-Aware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12771)
*Prateek Kumar Rajput,Abdoul Aziz Bonkoungou,Alberick Euraste Djiré,Xunzhu Tang,Yewei Song,Iyiola Emmanuel Olatunji,El Hacen Diallo,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文质疑了当前针对代码大语言模型记忆化（memorization）检测技术的有效性，指出传统探针（包括编码器式和解码器式）在大规模模型上会失效。通过可逆数学变换将"表征负荷"（representation load）与"记忆化"分离，发现大规模编码器能有效吸收表征负荷并收敛到正确解族。作者认为在实际软件工程中，适应不同表面形式的能力比"是否见过训练样本"更重要，呼吁未来评估应围绕分离这两种现象来设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献广泛报道代码LLM存在记忆化问题，但传统检测技术（如同义词模糊测试、死代码注入等扰动方法）在扩展到大规模模型时严重失效。作者希望理解为什么这些探针能干扰小模型却对大模型无效，并试图将表征负荷与记忆化从概念上分离，而不是将它们作为单一现象处理。

Method: 作者对数值问题应用可逆数学变换（invertible mathematical transforms）来隔离表征负荷与记忆化两个因素。通过这种方式，可以观察模型在改变表面形式但保持底层解结构不变的情况下，是否能正确收敛，从而区分模型是真正理解问题结构还是仅仅记忆了训练时的具体形式。

Result: 研究发现：1) 传统编码器式探针（如同义词模糊测试、死代码插入）和基于对数概率的解码器式探针在扩展规模时均性能退化，即使在已知污染的基准上也是如此；2) 大规模编码器能成功吸收大量的表征负荷，同时仍收敛到正确的解族；3) 记忆化虽然会虚增污染基准上的分数，但一旦剔除表征负荷因素，是否记忆过某个具体解就不再是关键问题。

Conclusion: 在实际软件工程应用中，模型适应不同表面形式的能力（表征负荷）才是决定可用性和泛化性的关键。记忆化本身不应成为过度担忧的对象，因为功能性答案即使最初是被记忆的，其价值仍然存在。未来的评估方法应围绕分离表征负荷与记忆化来构建，而非依赖将二者纠缠在一起的现有方法论。

Abstract: The extent to which large language models for code rely on memorization over genuine understanding remains highly debated. While current literature frequently reports widespread memorization, evaluating the underlying probing techniques across dense architectures reveals a severe breakdown in their utility at scale. Traditional encoder-style probes using perturbations such as synonym fuzzing or dead-code insertion struggle to expose memorization in scaled models, even on known-contaminated benchmarks, and decoder-style probes that rely on log probabilities show similar performance degradation. The specific mode of failure for these probes, particularly why such techniques disrupt smaller models but fail to impact larger ones, motivates us to untangle representation load from memorization rather than treating them as a single phenomenon. By applying invertible mathematical transforms to numeric problems, we isolate these two factors and reveal that scaled encoders successfully absorb substantial representation load while still converging on the correct family of solutions. In practical software engineering, this ability to adapt to varying surface forms is what truly matters for usability and generalizability in LLM and agentic applications. Whether a specific solution was seen during training becomes a much less pressing question because although memorization inflates scores on contaminated benchmarks, factoring out representation load makes it debatable how much we should truly care if a functional answer was originally memorized. Future evaluations must therefore be built around separating these phenomena rather than relying on methodologies that quietly entangle them.

</details>


### [168] [Dissecting Software Graphs: Structural Insights for Driver-Guided Fuzzing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12859)
*Baihong Chen,Hua Ming,Weifeng Pan,Tian Xie,Haipeng Cai,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对多驱动（multi-driver）模糊测试下的软件结构进行了实证研究，提出基于静态调用图投影动态覆盖的结构抽象方法，并在27个OSS-Fuzz C/C++项目上验证：多驱动模糊测试相比最佳单驱动基线，调用图节点覆盖提升27.9%，CFG边覆盖提升73.5%，并发现11个单驱动模糊测试遗漏的独特bug，同时揭示了驱动贡献不均、子图差异大及残余欠探索遵循规律性模式等结构性发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多软件系统通过命令行选项、子命令和配置标志暴露多种执行模式，这类程序的模糊测试同时依赖变异输入和所调用的模式。然而现有评估仍聚焦于覆盖率和bug数量，不清楚执行模式如何划分、重叠和遗漏软件结构，以及这些差异如何影响模糊测试的有效性。

Method: 提出一种结构抽象方法：以静态调用图作为共享骨干，将驱动特定动态覆盖投影到调用图上，导出驱动诱导子图。基于此开发了四阶段方法：骨干构建、模糊测试与剖析、基于图的分析，以及研究问题驱动的评估。

Result: 在27个OSS-Fuzz C/C++项目（涵盖43个可执行文件和854个驱动配置）上进行实验。在相同总预算下，多驱动模糊测试优于最佳单驱动基线：调用图节点覆盖提升27.9%，CFG边覆盖提升73.5%，发现11个单驱动模糊测试基本遗漏的独特bug和异常行为。但驱动贡献不均，子图在凝聚性、碎片化、模块化、重叠度方面差异显著，残余欠探索遵循重复出现的规律模式而非均匀尾部。

Conclusion: 结果表明多驱动模糊测试本质上是一个结构探索问题，执行模式对软件结构的划分、重叠和遗漏具有重大影响，这为未来设计更好的多驱动模糊测试策略提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Many software systems expose multiple execution modes through command-line options, subcommands, and configuration flags. For such programs, fuzzing depends on both mutated inputs and the invoked mode. Yet evaluations still focus on coverage and bug counts, leaving unclear how execution modes partition, overlap, and miss software structure, and how these differences affect effectiveness. We present an empirical study of software structure under multi-driver fuzzing. We propose a structural abstraction that uses a static call graph as a shared backbone and projects driver-specific dynamic coverage onto it to derive driver-induced subgraphs. Based on this abstraction, we develop a four-phase methodology for backbone construction, fuzzing and profiling, graph-based analysis, and research-question-driven evaluation. We apply it to 27 OSS-Fuzz-derived C/C++ projects, spanning 43 executables and 854 driver configurations. Under the same total budget, multi-driver fuzzing outperforms the best single-driver baseline, increasing covered call-graph nodes by 27.9% and CFG-edge coverage by 73.5%, and revealing 11 unique bugs and abnormal behaviors largely missed by single-driver fuzzing. However, driver contributions are uneven, subgraphs differ substantially in cohesion, fragmentation, modularity, overlap, and residual under-exploration follows recurring regimes rather than a homogeneous tail. These results show that multi-driver fuzzing is fundamentally a structural exploration problem.

</details>


### [169] [Requirements-Augmented Generation for Trustworthy Acceptance Testing of LLM-Based Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12970)
*Fanyu Wang,Chetan Arora,Zhenping Xie,Yonghui Liu,Kla Tantithamthavorn,Aldeida Aleti,Siwei Jiang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向LLM软件（LBS）的自动化验收测试框架，通过Requirements-Augmented Generation (REAG)生成上下文感知的测试预言，并结合基于置信度校准的级联判定方法（confidence-calibrated cascade judgment）来量化判定可靠性，在工业营养咨询应用上取得了98.8%的准确率和显著的成本效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统软件具有确定性输出，而LLM软件表现出上下文相关、随机的行为，导致经典的验收测试和测试预言方法不再适用。同一查询可能因用户角色和软件上下文不同而需要根本不同的响应，因此迫切需要能够自主解释用户指令并可靠推断用户意图的自动化验收测试框架。

Method: 该框架包含两项技术贡献：（1）Requirements-Augmented Generation (REAG)，通过自适应RAG和自推理检索相关软件需求、领域知识和用户角色来生成上下文感知的测试预言；（2）置信度校准的级联判定方法，通过模拟专家一致性来量化判定可靠性，接受高置信度判定、升级模糊案例或在不确定时弃权，并由共形风险控制提供经验可靠性保证。

Result: 在工业营养咨询应用的案例研究中，REAG达到3.91/5的预言质量评分，82%的案例达到合格或边缘预言质量。置信度校准级联判定达到98.8%的准确率，通过过滤不合格输出将预言质量从3.91提升至4.30，相比单一判定基线实现了31.7%的成本效率提升。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结合需求增强生成与置信度校准的级联判定，有效解决了LLM软件验收测试中意图推断和判定可靠性两大核心挑战，并通过工业案例验证了其实用价值和商业可行性。

Abstract: LLM-based software (LBS) integrates large language models as core components to deliver flexible, personalised responses. Unlike traditional software with deterministic outputs, LBSs exhibit context-dependent, stochastic behaviour that renders classical acceptance testing and test oracles insufficient: the same query may require fundamentally different responses depending on user personas and software context. This gap creates an urgent need for automated acceptance testing frameworks that autonomously interpret user instructions, while reliably inferring user intentions in a changing environment. In this paper, we present an automated acceptance testing framework for LBS with calibrated verdict reliability via two technical contributions. First, we introduce Requirements-Augmented Generation (REAG), which interprets user intentions by retrieving relevant software requirements, domain knowledge, and personas via adaptive RAG and self-reasoning to generate context-aware test oracles. Second, recognising that oracle generation may retrieve irrelevant constraints, misinterpret intent, or hallucinate requirements, we introduce a confidence-calibrated cascade judgment. This method quantifies verdict reliability via simulated expert agreement -- accepting high-confidence verdicts, escalating ambiguous cases, or abstaining when uncertain -- with empirical reliability guarantees backed by conformal risk control. An industrial case study on a production nutrition advisory application demonstrates that REAG achieves a 3.91/5 oracle quality score, reaching qualified or marginal oracle quality in 82% of cases. The confidence-calibrated cascade achieves 98.8% accuracy, improves oracle quality from 3.91 to 4.30 by filtering unqualified outputs, and delivers a 31.7% cost-efficiency improvement over single-judge baselines, validating industrial viability

</details>


### [170] [How Powerful are LLMs in Generating Formal Program Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13077)
*Fanpeng Yang,Xing Li,Shuling Wang,Jie An,Zeyu Sun,Shenghua Feng,Wenhan Wang,Weiyi Wang,Naijun Zhan,Fanjiang Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Coins，一个基于Rocq的形式化验证评估框架，通过在被评估的规约上实例化可信测试用例并生成具体证明义务来评估LLM生成的程序规约质量，避免传统方法将证明难度与规约质量混淆的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化验证需要编写精确的规约，成本高昂。虽然LLM在定理证明和验证代码生成方面表现出色，但其生成程序规约的能力尚不明确。现有评估方法要么需要验证实现一致性，要么需要证明规约间的语义等价，这两者都极其困难，且可能将证明难度与规约质量混为一谈。

Method: Coins框架利用形式推理的不对称性：成功的证明提供可靠证据，而证明失败本身具有模糊性。其核心方法是在可信测试用例上实例化待评估的规约，生成具体的证明义务，从而评估规约质量。作者在HumanEval上使用人工编写的Rocq规约进行了大规模研究。

Result: 研究发现规约生成仍然是一个艰巨的挑战，验证复杂度可能掩盖规约质量的真实差异。基于测试用例的形式化推理相比模型规模扩展，能提供更忠实和更有区分度的进展度量。

Conclusion: 准确的规约评估（而非单纯的模型规模扩展）是理解LLM规约合成能力的核心；基于测试用例的形式化推理是评估规约质量更可靠、更具区分度的方式。这一设计利用成功证明的证据可靠性，避免了对证明失败模糊性的依赖。

Abstract: Formal verification provides strong guarantees of software correctness, but its adoption is limited by the high cost of writing precise formal specifications. While recent large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in theorem proving and verified code generation, their true ability to generate program specifications remains unclear. Existing evaluations require either verifying implementation conformance or proving semantic equivalence between specifications, both of which are formidably difficult and may conflate proof difficulty with specification quality. To address this problem, we introduce Coins, a Rocq based evaluation framework that assesses specification quality by instantiating specifications under evaluation on trusted test cases and generating concrete proof obligations. This design aligns with the asymmetric nature of formal reasoning, where successful proofs provide reliable evidence while proof failures are inherently ambiguous. Using Coins, we conduct a large scale study on HumanEval with a curated set of human written Rocq specifications. Our results show that specification generation remains a formidable challenge, and that verification complexity can obscure genuine differences in specification quality. Overall, we find that accurate specification evaluation, rather than model scaling alone, is central to understanding the power of LLMs for specification synthesis, and that test case based formal reasoning offers a more faithful and discriminative measure of progress.

</details>


### [171] [Can Formal Specifications Be Synthesized from Tests Alone?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13240)
*Tianhai Liu,Maximilian Müller,Tobias Hey,Vitus Lüntzel,Muhammad Minhas,Anne Koziolek,Bernhard Beckert*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于LLM的黑盒规范推断方法：仅通过测试代码和动态执行轨迹（程序接口、输入输出、状态变化）来生成候选JML规范，利用有界模型检查进行本地验证和迭代精化，避免了白盒访问带来的知识产权和部署成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化规范能提供强保证，但手工编写成本高昂。现有LLM方法依赖白盒源码访问，在工业应用中面临知识产权泄露风险和部署成本障碍，需要探索仅基于黑盒信息（测试代码和动态执行轨迹）的规范推断方法。

Method: LLM仅观察程序接口、选定输入及对应的输出/状态变化（实现内部不可见），据此推断候选规范；随后使用有界模型检查在本地验证候选规范，并将检查反馈用于迭代精化。

Result: 在SpecGenBench基准上的初步结果表明，测试代码能够引导LLM生成有意义的JML规范；同时也揭示了检查器兼容性和诊断反馈是可靠迭代精化的关键挑战。

Conclusion: 黑盒规范推断是可行的研究方向，但需要在检查器兼容性和反馈诊断质量上进一步改进，才能实现可靠的规范精化流程。

Abstract: Formal specifications offer strong guarantees, but remain costly to write manually. Recent LLM-based approaches automate this by inferring specifications from source code, yet their reliance on white-box access poses barriers to industrial adoption due to intellectual property risks and deployment costs. Our approach uses LLMs to infer candidate specifications solely from test code and dynamic execution traces: the LLM observes only the program interface, selected inputs, and corresponding outputs or state changes, while the implementation internals remain hidden. Candidate specifications are validated locally using bounded model checking, with feedback guiding iterative refinement. Initial results on the SpecGenBench benchmark suggest that tests can guide LLMs towards meaningful Java Modeling Language specifications, while also highlighting checker compatibility and diagnostic feedback as key challenges for reliable refinement.

</details>


### [172] [Refine After Generation: Toward Correct and Concise Patches in LLM-based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13292)
*Wenqiang Luo,Jacky Keung,Xiaoyu Shi,Yicheng Sun,Boyang Yang,Zhou Yang,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM自动程序修复中补丁冗长性（patch verbosity）这一被忽视的问题，并提出RECAP——一个轻量级、即插即用的后处理适配器，通过监督微调和直接偏好优化训练的精炼器，在不牺牲修复能力的前提下大幅降低补丁大小和复杂度，实现更好的size-correctness权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在自动程序修复中已能解决真实仓库级问题，但评估只关注是否通过测试，忽视补丁本身的质量。作者发现成功补丁普遍比开发者补丁更大更复杂，这种冗长性源于能力导向的设计选择（如迭代精炼和广泛上下文），表面控制（如输出格式或最小化提示）难以改善，这影响代码可审查性和可维护性。

Method: 作者首先对SWE-bench Verified上的28种SOTA方法进行系统性分析，量化补丁冗长性问题。然后提出RECAP框架：将补丁最小化与生成解耦，在生成后附加轻量级精炼器。精炼器通过监督微调（SFT）和直接偏好优化（DPO）训练，使用蒸馏推理轨迹和从多来源构建的补丁对数据集。作为即插即用适配器，可挂接到现有修复框架之后。

Result: 在四个宿主系统上，RECAP将平均总变更从+242.14%降至+4.24%，净变更从+348.24%降至-39.75%（相对开发者补丁），同时修复数量保持或提升多达42个实例。而提示、提交拆解和最小化感知等基线方法在减少补丁大小时牺牲了49到217个已解决实例。

Conclusion: 最小化不能简单地归结为句法压缩；将最小化与生成解耦为获得更可审查的修复提供了实用路径。RECAP证明了在不牺牲正确性的前提下实现补丁最小化是可行的，对LLM-based APR的评估标准提出了扩展需求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have advanced automatic program repair (APR) to the point where agentic systems routinely resolve real-world, repository-level issues. Yet the generated patch has received little scrutiny beyond whether it passes tests.
  In this paper, we identify patch verbosity as a major yet overlooked concern in LLM-based APR. Characterizing 28 state-of-the-art approaches on SWE-bench Verified, we find that even successful patches are consistently larger and more complex than developer patches, with the median approach producing 121.78% more total changes, 80.91% more net changes, and 43.99% higher cyclomatic complexity. We further show that this verbosity is rooted in capability-oriented design choices such as iterative refinement and broad context, and can hardly be reduced by surface-level controls such as output format or minimality prompts. Motivated by these findings, we formulate post-generation patch refinement and propose RECAP, a lightweight, plug-and-play adapter that attaches to existing repair frameworks after generation. RECAP's refiner is trained via supervised fine-tuning and direct preference optimization with distilled reasoning traces, on a dataset of patch pairs we construct from multiple sources. Across four host systems, prompting, commit-untangling, and minimality-aware baselines reduce patch size only by sacrificing 49 to 217 resolved instances. In contrast, RECAP achieves a substantially better size-correctness tradeoff, cutting average total changes from +242.14% to +4.24% and net changes from +348.24% to -39.75% relative to developer patches while preserving or improving resolution by up to 42 instances. Our results indicate that minimality cannot be simply reduced to syntactic compression, and that decoupling minimization from generation offers a practical path to more reviewable repairs.

</details>


### [173] [Integration-First Structural Coverage for Embedded Software:Trace-Based Evidence, Hybrid Runtime Analysis, and Cross-Variant Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13322)
*Alexander Weiss,Albert Schulz,Michael Wittner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对嵌入式项目测试中单元级覆盖率与集成/系统级覆盖率之间的不匹配问题，提出了集成优先的覆盖率策略、基于嵌入式跟踪和混合运行时分析（hRA）的覆盖率测量技术，以及Hyper Coverage证据整合层，使覆盖率测量在优化编译（-O3）的可发布二进制上依然可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入式项目中覆盖率主要在单元级别收集，因为该级别插桩和可观测性成本低，但最有代表性的完整性信号应来自设备上的集成和系统测试。经典插桩会扰动时序、内存占用和并发行为，而纯迹线重建的覆盖率在编译器激进优化下对决策和条件的可靠性会下降，形成覆盖信号不匹配的问题。

Method: 从过程和技术的两端同时解决。过程端提出集成优先覆盖率策略，将集成和系统测试作为基线测量，通过显式闭包循环驱动残余缺口，使完整性建立在"已覆盖或已论证"而非"仅覆盖"之上。技术端使用嵌入式跟踪作为观测路径，加入混合运行时分析（hRA）：一种最小化、保持语义的可观测性脚手架，在-O3优化构建的跟踪流中保持决策和条件边界可区分，同时所有覆盖状态和计数保持在目标设备之外。最后提出Hyper Coverage整合层，合并跨测试级别、测试运行、变体和构建配置的证据，暴露在所有相关变体中均未测试的源码行。

Result: 该方法将对象到源代码的映射从启发式重建转变为可审查的证据，使分支、条件和MC/DC测量在类似发布的二进制文件上变得实用，并能识别在所有变体中始终未被测试的代码行。

Conclusion: 通过集成优先策略、hRA技术支撑和Hyper Coverage整合层，论文实现了嵌入式系统中更高代表性和更可靠的结构覆盖率测量，弥合了单元级与集成/系统级覆盖率之间的差距。

Abstract: Structural coverage is widely used as evidence that testing is complete, yet in embedded projects it is predominantly collected at unit level, simply because that is where instrumentation and observability are inexpensive. This produces a mismatch.
