The most representative completeness signal would come from integration and system tests executed on the device under test, but classical instrumentation perturbs timing, memory footprint and concurrency behaviour, while purely trace-reconstructed coverage loses reliability for decisions and conditions as soon as the compiler optimizes aggressively. We address this mismatch from both ends. On the process side we describe an integrationfirst coverage strategy that treats integration and system tests as the baseline measurement and drives the residual gaps through an explicit closure loop, so that completeness is established as covered or justified rather than as covered alone. On the technical side we use embedded trace as the observation path and add hybrid runtime analysis (hRA): a minimal, semantics-preserving observability scaffolding that keeps decision and condition boundaries distinguishable in the trace stream of an optimized (-O3) build, while all coverage state and counting remain off-target. This converts object-to-source mapping from a heuristic reconstruction into reviewable evidence and makes branch, condition and MC/DC measurement practical on release-like binaries. Finally we describe Hyper Coverage, a consolidation layer that merges evidence across test levels, test runs, variants and build configurations, and that exposes source lines which remain untested in every relevant variant.

</details>


### [174] [Does Fixing Break Security? An Empirical Study of Security Degradation in Iterative LLM-Driven Infrastructure-as-Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13404)
*Benjamin Agyekum,Fabio Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究分析了迭代LLM修复IaC(基础设施即代码)过程中的安全回归现象。通过IaC-Eval基准的5,968条场景时间线、4,440次迭代转换，追踪30个CIS检查项。结果显示：严格检测模式下3.3%的场景(5.2%的转换)出现安全回归，主要根因是资源重构(79.0%)；回归转换表现出更高的代码变动量和检查波动性；36.6%的标准模式回归可在平均1.2次迭代内自愈，第3次迭代是最佳停止点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅报告累积最佳指标（非递减），从未检视IaC迭代LLM修复过程中单次迭代的安全轨迹。作者希望探究迭代修复在修复其他问题的同时是否会导致安全退化，填补这一研究空白。

Method: 使用IaC-Eval基准的5,968条场景时间线（每条场景运行至多5次修复迭代），覆盖15种配置（6种模型特定RAG、9种模型聚合非RAG、每种3个温度），产生4,440次两侧均有Checkov数据的迭代转换。追踪30个CIS检查ID，通过代码差异分类根因，并在标准（包容）与严格（排除性检查失败）两种检测模式下分析。

Result: 标准检测下13.8%场景(24.8%转换)出现至少一次回归；严格检测下降至3.3%场景(5.2%转换)。资源重构(79.0%)是主导根因。回归转换表现出2.6倍更多代码变动(Cohen's d=0.90)和4.9倍更高的严格模式检查波动性(d=1.49)。36.6%的标准模式回归平均在1.2次迭代内自愈；第3次迭代为最优停止点。

Conclusion: 迭代式IaC修复确实会引入安全回归，但保守且可辩护的比率约为3.3%场景。研究结果支持安全感知的反馈环路设计，并提供可操作的迭代预算指导。

Abstract: Background: Iterative feedback loops are the dominant paradigm for improving LLM-generated Infrastructure-as-Code (IaC): validators such as Checkov and terraform validate feed error signals back for successive repair attempts. Prior work reports cumulative-best metrics, which are non-decreasing by construction, so the raw per-iteration security trajectory has never been examined for IaC. Aims: We study security regression (a previously-passing CIS Benchmark check that fails after a repair iteration) to determine whether and how often iterative LLM repair degrades security while fixing other issues. Method: We analyze 5,968 scenario timelines from the IaC-Eval benchmark, each one scenario run through one configuration for up to 5 repair iterations. The 15 configurations (six model-specific RAG, nine model-aggregated non-RAG, three temperatures each) yield 4,440 iteration transitions with Checkov data on both sides. We track 30 individual CIS check IDs and classify root causes from code diffs, under two detection modes: standard (inclusive) and strict (exclusive check failures only). Results: Under standard detection, 13.8% of scenarios (24.8% of transitions) exhibit at least one regression. Under strict detection the rate falls to 3.3% of scenarios (5.2% of transitions), indicating most apparent regressions are multi-resource measurement artifacts. Resource restructuring (79.0%) is the dominant root cause. Regression transitions show 2.6x more code churn (Cohen's d=0.90) and 4.9x higher strict-mode check volatility (d=1.49). Of standard-mode regressions, 36.6% self-correct within an average of 1.2 iterations; iteration 3 is the optimal stopping point. Conclusions: Iterative IaC repair does introduce security regressions, but the conservative, defensible rate is about 3.3% of scenarios. Our findings motivate security-aware feedback-loop design and actionable iteration-budget guidance.

</details>


### [175] [LLM-Assisted Dynamic Threat Analysis for Attacker-Reachable Software Weaknesses in Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13450)
*Md Wasiul Haque,Sagar Dasgupta,Mizanur Rahman,Md Rayhanur Rahman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究使用大语言模型（LLM）自动化构建Autoware自动驾驶软件栈的动态分析测试工件。通过编译器精确静态分析识别出1375条决策规则、2274个验证检查和482条输入到安全输出的数据流，从中采样740个可达弱点位置。两个本地开源LLM生成了3700个工件集，编译测试发现80%的首次编译失败源于依赖连接而非程序逻辑。推理模型首次编译成功率为64%，而代码专用模型仅为6%。尽管修复后实现了完全可编译，但不到一半的harness进入模糊测试，所有37个崩溃都源自stub代码而非Autoware本身，最终未能在预算内动态确认任何候选弱点。结论是构建集成（build integration）而非候选生成或模糊测试，是阻碍LLM辅助动态分析全栈自动驾驶软件的主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统依赖大规模安全关键软件栈，从对抗性输入触达的弱点可能影响转向、制动等控制决策。静态分析能识别候选弱点位置，但动态确认可利用性需要可执行测试工件，而手工构造这些工件极其困难。作者希望探索LLM能否自动化这一过程。

Method: 1) 对Autoware的185个包进行编译器精确静态分析，提取决策规则、验证检查和输入到安全输出的数据流，构建弱点分类法并采样740个可达位置；2) 使用两个本地开源LLM（一个推理模型、一个代码专用模型）生成测试harness工件集，同时设置无静态上下文消融和朴素模板基线；3) 将生成的工件在真实构建下用sanitizers编译，通过编译器反馈循环进行修复；4) 对可执行工件进行模糊测试。共生成3700个工件集。

Result: 主要结果包括：1) 构建集成失败分类法显示80%的首次编译失败源于依赖连接而非程序逻辑；2) 推理模型首次编译成功率为64%，代码专用模型仅6%；3) 仅推理模型通过大量stubbing实现了完全对象可编译性；4) 但不到一半的harness进入模糊测试阶段；5) 所有37个观察到的崩溃均源自stub代码而非Autoware本身；6) 在预算内没有任何候选弱点被动态确认。

Conclusion: 构建集成（build integration）是可靠地进行LLM辅助全栈自动驾驶软件动态分析的主要障碍，而非候选生成或模糊测试环节。这一发现对利用LLM辅助安全关键系统动态分析的未来研究方向具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles depend on large safety-critical software stacks, where weaknesses reachable from adversarial inputs may affect steering, braking, or other control decisions. Static analysis can identify candidate sites, but dynamically confirming exploitability requires executable test artifacts that are difficult to construct manually. We investigate whether large language models (LLMs) can automate this process for Autoware, an open-source autonomous-driving stack. We perform compiler-precise static analysis across 185 packages, identifying 1,375 decision rules, 2,274 validation checks, and 482 input-to-safety-output flows, from which we derive a weakness taxonomy and sample 740 reachable sites. Two local open-weight LLMs, a no-static-context ablation, and a naive-template baseline generate 3,700 artifact sets, which are compiled against the real build under sanitizers, repaired through compiler-in-the-loop feedback, and fuzzed when executable. The main result is a build-integration failure taxonomy showing that 80% of first-shot compilation failures arise from dependency wiring rather than program logic. The reasoning model compiled 64% of harnesses on the first attempt, compared with 6% for the code-specialized model. Repair achieved full object-compileability for the reasoning model only through extensive stubbing; fewer than half of its harnesses reached the fuzzer, and all 37 observed crashes originated in stubbed code rather than Autoware. No candidate weakness was dynamically confirmed within budget. These results show that build integration, not candidate generation or fuzzing, is the primary barrier to reliable LLM-assisted dynamic analysis of full autonomous-vehicle software stacks.

</details>


### [176] [CAPRI: Contract-Aware Proof Repair for Isabelle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13459)
*Jim Woodcock,Gabriel Leite,Augusto Sampaio,Ran Wei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPRI，一个合约感知的Isabelle证明修复工作流，由Isabelle验证证明、独立检查器强制执行机器可读的编辑合约，并保留完整审计轨迹。在180次运行中产生138个有效修复，证明了仅限证明体的界面在避免合约违规方面更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型辅助Isabelle证明发现时，Isabelle构建只能确认提交的理论被接受，无法验证LLM只修改了开发者授权的部分。需要确保LLM的编辑行为符合开发者设定的合约约束。

Method: 提出CAPRI合约感知修复工作流：Isabelle负责证明检查，独立的检查器强制执行机器可读的编辑合约。保留提示词、提议、候选仓库、诊断、判定和哈希以供审计。评估五种工作流，包含全理论编辑与仅证明体编辑等不同界面，并在不同模型配置（OpenAI Responses、Luna、Sol）下进行对比。

Result: 在144个被Isabelle接受的最终候选中，6个修改了受保护文本，均出现在可编辑完整理论的迭代工作流中。仅证明体界面产生29/36有效修复且无合约违规，而对应的全理论工作流为31/36。一次性修复为22/36，冻结的迭代工作流为32/36。Sol配置产生33/36修复，比OpenAI Responses的29/36好，但McNemar检验（p=0.0625）表明差异不显著。

Conclusion: 限制LLM可编辑的内容（仅证明体而非完整理论）可以有效消除合约违规，代价是略微降低修复成功率。不同模型配置间的性能差异未达到统计显著性。

Abstract: We address the use of large language models (LLMs) to help discover Isabelle proofs. An Isabelle build establishes that the submitted theory is accepted, but not that an LLM changed only what the developer authorised. We present CAPRI, a contract-aware repair workflow in which Isabelle checks the proof and an independent checker enforces a machine-readable edit contract. Prompts, proposals, candidate repositories, diagnostics, verdicts, and hashes are retained for audit. We evaluate five workflows on twelve failed proofs from four developments, with three replicates per task and condition, giving 180 runs and 138 valid repairs. Of 144 terminal candidates accepted by Isabelle, six had modified protected text; all arose in iterative workflows that could edit a complete theory. A proof-body-only interface produced 29/36 valid repairs and no contract violations, compared with 31/36 for the corresponding full-theory workflow. One-shot repair produced 22/36, while a later prospectively frozen iterative workflow produced 32/36; these figures compare complete workflows rather than individual mechanisms. A separate post hoc OpenRouter campaign found no improvement in the designated Luna comparisons. A Sol configuration with matched demonstrations produced 33/36 repairs, compared with 29/36 in the frozen OpenAI Responses condition, but the difference was not statistically significant in a one-sided exact McNemar test ($p=0.0625$).

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [DualPI2 Active Queue Management in ns-3: Implementation And Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12513)
*Maria Eduarda Veras,Eduardo Freitas,Assis T. de Oliveira Filho,Djamel Sadok,Judith Kelner*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文在ns-3网络模拟器中实现了L4S标准核心组件DualPI2 AQM，镜像Linux内核架构，包含了此前模拟中缺失的机制（如基于信用的WRR调度、步进标记和过载保护），并在25种BDP场景下与物理Linux测试平台验证，证明模型能准确复现真实行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L4S标准正被广泛采用，但其核心组件DualPI2 AQM在ns-3模拟器中缺乏高保真、最新的模型，这阻碍了研究者对L4S性能的评估与改进。

Method: 在ns-3中全面实现DualPI2 AQM，镜像官方Linux内核架构，纳入基于信用的WRR调度、步进标记和过载保护等此前缺失的代表性机制，并通过物理Linux测试平台在25种BDP场景下进行验证。

Result: ns-3模型能够复现真实世界行为，确保严格的吞吐量公平性和队列延迟隔离，在25种BDP场景下与Linux测试平台结果一致。

Conclusion: 该验证过的模型为研究社区提供了稳健工具，可在从数据中心到无线家庭和办公环境的多种网络拓扑中评估和推进L4S性能。

Abstract: The demand for ultra-low latency applications necessitates advanced network architectures like the Low Latency, Low Loss, and Scalable Throughput (L4S) standard. A core component of L4S is the DualPI2 Active Queue Management (AQM), which ensures the safe coexistence of scalable and classic traffic. Despite L4S's growing adoption, the ns-3 network simulator lacks a high-fidelity, up-to-date DualPI2 model. This paper presents a comprehensive implementation of the DualPI2 AQM in ns-3, while also mirroring the official Linux Kernel architecture. Our model incorporates representative mechanisms previously absent in simulation, such as credit-based Weighted Round Robin (WRR) scheduling, step-marking, and overload protection. To guarantee simulation accuracy, we validate our implementation against a physical Linux testbed across 25 diverse Bandwidth-Delay Product (BDP) scenarios. Results demonstrate that our ns-3 model replicates real-world behavior, ensuring strict throughput fairness and queue delay isolation. Ultimately, this validated model equips the research community with a robust tool to evaluate and advance L4S performance across diverse network topologies, ranging from data centers to wireless home and office environments.

</details>


### [178] [Digital Twin Satellite Networks: A Paradigm for Intelligent, Efficient, and Resilient Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12865)
*Mustafa Alhassan,Peng Hu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个数字孪生卫星网络（DTSN）框架，作为闭环架构用于LEO卫星星座的可靠与智能管理，通过物理网络与虚拟孪生的同步连接，结合实时遥测、通感一体化（ISAC）、预测智能和韧性导向控制，并在NASA 42航天器模拟器和Python DT桥接的跨域协同仿真中验证了其隔离受损节点和主动网络重构的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO巨型星座卫星网络面临拓扑快速变化、星间链路间歇性中断、硬件扰动及SWaP约束等严峻挑战。现有基于DT、DTN、SDN和O-RAN的方案虽提供了构建模块，但未能完全支持实时、预测性和平台感知的网络运行，需要一种更全面的闭环管理体系。

Method: 提出DTSN框架，作为连接物理卫星网络与同步虚拟孪生的闭环架构。框架整合四部分：实时遥测、通感一体化（ISAC）、预测性智能以及韧性导向控制。通过NASA 42航天器模拟器与Python DT桥接实现星座级跨域协同仿真，利用预测前视机制和指数传感器恢复模型来管理包括运动漂移、硬件故障和对抗性干扰在内的多域威胁环境。

Result: 在600秒飞行窗口内，DTSN框架成功隔离了受损节点并触发主动网络重构，实现了无中断服务和动态网络韧性，验证了框架在预测性和韧性导向卫星网络运营方面的有效性。

Conclusion: DTSN展示了支撑预测性和韧性导向卫星网络运营的潜力，为LEO卫星星座的智能管理提供了一种可行的闭环解决方案，填补了现有方法在实时预测和平台感知方面的空白。

Abstract: Satellite mega-constellations in Low Earth Orbit (LEO) are becoming an important part of next-generation non-terrestrial networks, but their operation remains challenging because of fast network topology variation, intermittent inter-satellite links, hardware disturbances, and strict Size, Weight, and Power (SWaP) constraints. Existing approaches based on Digital Twin (DT), Digital Twin Network (DTN), Software-Defined Networking (SDN), and Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) provide useful building blocks for intelligent satellite networking, but they do not fully support real-time, predictive, and platform-aware network operation. In this paper, we propose a Digital Twin Satellite Network (DTSN) framework as a closed-loop architecture for reliable and intelligent management of LEO satellite constellations. The proposed framework connects the physical satellite network with a synchronized virtual twin and combines real-time telemetry, Integrated Sensing and Communication (ISAC), predictive intelligence, and resilience-oriented control. To validate the concept, we develop a constellation-scale cross-domain co-simulation using the NASA 42 spacecraft simulator and a Python-based DT bridge for a LEO constellation. The DT continuously ingests physical telemetry to manage a multi-domain threat environment, encompassing kinematic drift, hardware failures, and adversarial jamming over a 600-second flight window. By leveraging a predictive lookahead mechanism and an exponential sensor recovery model, the framework successfully isolates compromised nodes and triggers proactive network reconfiguration, thereby ensuring uninterrupted service and dynamic network resilience. These results show the potential of DTSN to support predictive and resilience-oriented satellite network operations.

</details>


### [179] [Pareto-Aware Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Online Resource Allocation in RIS-assisted Large-Scale IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13032)
*Wenhan Xu,Jiashuo Jiang,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAAERL框架，一种面向RIS辅助物联网网络的降维分层强化学习方案，通过引入Pareto感知的低维权重向量替代高维RIS波束成形变量，并集成自编码器进行二次数据驱动压缩，从而在保持Pareto最优前沿信息不丢失的前提下显著降低计算复杂度，大幅缩短训练时间并降低网络成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS辅助多用户系统的优化通常涉及高维物理层变量和非凸的Pareto最优速率集，导致实时应用面临严重的计算挑战，现有方法难以在密集网络中高效求解。

Method: 1) 将高维连续RIS波束成形变量替换为低维权重向量，严格表示Pareto最优前沿，理论上避免凸与非凸速率区域中的几何信息丢失；2) 集成自编码器架构执行二次数据驱动压缩，将优先级空间映射到高度浓缩的连续潜在动作空间；3) 构建分层强化学习框架进行在线资源分配优化。

Result: 在多用户移动边缘计算（MEC）网络等实际通信场景的广泛仿真中，PAAERL大幅减少了离线训练时间，加速了在线策略收敛，并显著降低了整体网络成本，优于现有最先进基准方法。

Conclusion: PAAERL展现出卓越的可扩展性和实际可行性，为下一代智能IoT环境中的实时RIS资源分配提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid evolution of 5G and emerging 6G networks, reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS) have become a critical technology for enhancing wireless communication scenarios. However, optimizing RIS-assisted multi-user systems typically introduces high-dimensional physical layer variables and non-convex Pareto-optimal rate sets, posing severe computational challenges for real-time applications. To address these limitations, this paper proposes a dimension-reduced, hierarchical reinforcement learning (RL) framework, termed Pareto-aware autoencoder-assisted RL (PAAERL), to optimize online resource allocation in RIS-assisted Internet of Things (IoT) networks. Our approach first substitutes high-dimensional continuous RIS beamforming variables with lower-dimensional weight vectors that strictly represent the Pareto-optimal frontier, theoretically avoiding geometric information loss across both convex and non-convex rate regions. To further mitigate the curse of dimensionality in dense networks, an autoencoder architecture is integrated to execute a secondary, data-driven compression phase, mapping the priority space into a highly condensed continuous latent action space. Extensive simulations conducted across practical communication scenarios, including multi-user mobile edge computing (MEC) networks, demonstrate that the proposed PAAERL framework drastically reduces offline training times, accelerates online policy convergence, and significantly decreases overall network costs compared to state-of-the-art benchmarks, underscoring its exceptional scalability and practical viability for next-generation intelligent IoT environments.

</details>


### [180] [Radio-Optical Confluence in Intelligent Edge Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13098)
*Akshita Gupta,Devika Dass,Agastya Raj,Carlos Natalino,Marco Ruffini,Paolo Monti,Daniel Kilper*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了无线接入网（RAN）致密化带来的挑战，并审视了超越无线与光域融合、走向全面融合这一方向的最新进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线接入网络致密化带来了效率与可扩展性方面的挑战，需要探索更高效、更具可扩展性的架构方案。

Method: 审视RAN致密化的挑战，并分析从无线与光融合迈向全面融合这一演进方向的最新研究进展。

Result: 未在摘要中明确给出具体实验结果，主要聚焦于对融合架构方向的进展回顾与分析。

Conclusion: 无线与光学从简单融合走向全面融合是应对RAN致密化挑战的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: Challenges associated with densification of radio access networks are motivating exploration of more efficient and scalable architectures. We examine recent progress in one direction that involves moving beyond radio and optical convergence to full confluence.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [181] [Position: Reasoning is a Learnable Rule-Based Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12325)
*Rachel Lawrence,Jacqueline Maasch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文（position paper），指出生成式AI社区在"推理"（reasoning）的定义上缺乏共识，导致推理评估的构念效度（construct validity）无法验证，阻碍了可信自主推理的可量化进展。作者提出基于文献综合的操作性定义，将有效且可靠的推理定位为一种可学习的基于规则的过程，并提供了一份研究报告传播的最佳实践清单。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在自主推理方面进展迅速，但社区对"推理"的操作性定义未达成共识，且常隐含地拒绝逻辑学和可验证自动推理领域对这一主题的历史性处理方式。这种定义上的模糊性使得推理评估的构念效度不可验证，从而削弱了向可信自主推理可量化进步的基础。

Method: 本文不提出新的模型或算法，而是：（1）综合文献，提出基于共识的操作性定义，将"有效且合理的推理"（valid and sound reasoning）定位为一种可学习的、基于规则的过程；（2）制定一份关于AI推理研究传播最佳实践的检查清单。

Result: 提供了两个主要贡献：一是合成文献的操作性定义，将推理重新框定为可学习的规则基础过程；二是提出用于AI推理研究沟通的最佳实践检查清单。

Conclusion: 定义歧义是可以解决的。通过明确操作性定义并遵循最佳实践清单，可以恢复推理评估的构念效度，从而推动生成式AI在可信自主推理方向上的可量化进展。

Abstract: Autonomous reasoning is among the most scientifically and economically motivating topics in AI today. Historically the purview of symbolic AI, recent advances have mainly emerged from deep probabilistic generative models. Despite immense interest and rapid progress, the generative AI community has not clearly converged on operational definitions for reasoning and often implicitly rejects the historical treatment of this topic in logic and verifiable automated reasoning. This position contends that definitional ambiguity leaves the construct validity of reasoning evaluation unverifiable, undermining quantifiable progress toward trustworthy autonomous reasoning. We also contend that this ambiguity is addressable. To that end, we provide (1) operational definitions based on a synthesis of the literature, positioning valid and sound reasoning as a learnable rule-based process; and (2) a checklist for best practices in the communication of AI reasoning research.

</details>


### [182] [Diagnostic Foundation for Evaluating LLMs' Research Integrity as Co-Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12345)
*Yash Tripathi,Silu Sharma,Sai Sidhanth Manoharan Jayanthi,Shivank Garg,Lin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IntegrityBench基准，评估语言模型在机构压力下维护科研诚信的能力，覆盖36个配对任务、5级隐显压力协议、3个领域和4个研究阶段。在18个前沿模型变体上，峰值压力下模型约有1/3的诚信关键决策失败，且模型规模和推理能力无法可靠缓解这一缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型越来越多地被用作共同科学家，但其在机构压力下维护科研诚信的能力尚未被量化测量，存在研究空白。

Method: 构建IntegrityBench基准，包含三个评估维度（不当行为分类、伦理行动推理、基于证据的决策制定），采用5级隐显压力协议，覆盖3个领域和4个研究阶段，对18个前沿模型变体进行系统评估。

Result: 峰值压力下模型约1/3的诚信关键决策失败；模型规模与推理能力不能可靠缓解；显式压力引发不当行为顺从，隐式情境重构导致对合法研究任务的过度拒绝；在不准确分类时，基于证据的决策表现反而不差（85.7 vs 79.4）。

Conclusion: 三个诚信评估维度在结构上相互分离，正确的伦理行动并不需要准确分类。前沿模型可能表面有帮助但暗藏诚信缺陷，产生两类部署风险：助长科研不当行为和侵蚀AI辅助研究的信任。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly deployed as co-scientists, yet their ability to uphold research integrity under institutional pressure remains unmeasured. We introduce IntegrityBench, a benchmark evaluating misconduct classification, ethical action reasoning and artifact-grounded decision making across 36 paired tasks under a 5-level implicit-explicit pressure protocol spanning 3 domains and 4 research stages. Evaluating 18 frontier model variants, we find that under peak pressure, models fail roughly 1 in 3 integrity-critical decisions, and neither scale nor reasoning ability reliably mitigates this. Explicit pressures induce compliance with misconduct, while implicit contextual reframing more often causes over-refusal of legitimate research tasks. Interestingly, models failing to classify research requests accurately perform equally or better on artifact-grounded decision making (85.7 vs. 79.4), suggesting the three facets are structurally dissociated and correct ethical action does not require accurate classification. Frontier models can thus appear helpful while harbouring integrity failures that create two distinct deployment risks: facilitating research misconduct and eroding trust in AI-assisted research.

</details>


### [183] [Position: The Alignment Community is Unintentionally Building a Censor's Toolkit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12346)
*Sarah Ball,Phil Hackemann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出观点：现代AI对齐方法（原本用于防止有害输出）是双重用途技术，可能被恶意行为者滥用于审查和操纵。随着AI作为信息提供者的快速普及、经济权力不对称以及政治环境趋向威权化，这种风险加剧，亟需讨论并制定缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为对齐技术虽然旨在让AI安全可靠，但其本质是控制模型输出的手段，这种控制能力同样可以被恶意行为者（尤其是威权政府）用来进行信息审查和舆论操纵，从而获得信息主导权。

Method: 采用立场论文（position paper）方式，通过映射当前对齐技术与可能的滥用例，论证对齐方法的双重用途性质。

Result: 指出"完美对齐"模型的追求在客观上为恶意行为者提供了不断改进的信息控制工具，并识别了加速该风险的三大因素：AI作为信息提供者的快速采纳、经济权力不对称、政治趋向威权化。

Conclusion: 呼吁社区正视AI对齐机制的故意滥用问题，并提出缓解策略以防范这种双重用途潜力。

Abstract: This position paper argues that modern AI alignment methods - originally designed to prevent harmful output - are dual-use technologies that may easily be misused by malicious actors for censorship and manipulation. By mapping current alignment techniques to the possibility and actual cases of misuse, we show that the quest for a "perfectly aligned" model inadvertently also provides malicious actors with an ever-improving tool for informational dominance. We need to discuss this dual-use potential now, as its risk is exacerbated by rapid user adoption of AI as information provider, economic power asymmetries, and a political landscape that increasingly shifts towards authoritarianism. We conclude by urging the community to consider the intentional misuse of AI alignment mechanisms and propose mitigation strategies to safeguard against this dual-use potential.

</details>


### [184] [TopoIntent: Compiling Security Intent into Executable, Compliance-Checked Network Topologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13389)
*Xiaokang Qu,Jianliang Ma,Zao Fan,Tianshu Chu,Tianlong Fan,Linyuan Lü*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TopoIntent是一个将自然语言安全意图编译为可执行、合规检查的网络拓扑的系统。它通过schema契约约束生成、密集向量检索参考架构、分阶段融合意图与模板，并对生成的拓扑进行CIS Controls v8.1.2合规检查，最后导出为带iptables ACL的Mininet脚本。在自建基准上，结构修复将拓扑级CIS满足率从0.78提升到1.00，反馈轮次将ACL策略通过率从0.78提升到0.88。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NetOps自动化工具主要在安全拓扑设计完成后运行，缺乏从欠规范的自然语言需求直接生成结构化安全拓扑的能力。企业安全拓扑设计需要将业务意图、法规要求和风险假设转化为具体的安全域、边界设备和访问控制策略，这一过程目前缺乏自动化支持。

Method: TopoIntent系统采用以下方法：(1) 使用schema契约约束拓扑生成；(2) 通过密集向量检索从精选模板库中获取参考架构；(3) 应用分阶段融合实现意图-模板对齐和安全补全；(4) 对照CIS Controls v8.1.2进行合规检查；(5) 通过加法式schema保留编辑修复结构缺口；(6) 将最终拓扑导出为带内核级iptables ACL的Mininet脚本以支持可执行测试。

Result: 在自建评估集（含22个模板和44个合成意图作为检索集，7个模板和14个意图作为金融/政府场景的留出集）上，加法式修复在平均少于1.5轮内将拓扑可见的CIS满足率从0.78提升到1.00；一次反馈轮次将ACL后策略通过率从0.78提升到0.88。

Conclusion: TopoIntent验证了从自然语言安全意图到可执行、合规检查的拓扑的自动化编译是可行的，加法式结构修复和反馈循环能有效提升合规性和策略通过率。该系统填补了需求到拓扑生成任务的空白，但由于领域没有公共基准，评估集为自建，且拓扑级CIS可见性有限，未解决案例仍需人工审查。

Abstract: Enterprise security topology design requires translating business intent, regulatory requirements, and risk assumptions into zones, boundary devices, inter-zone paths, and access-control policies. Existing NetOps automation tools mainly operate after this design is fixed, providing limited support for generating structured security topologies from underspecified natural-language requirements. We present TopoIntent, a system that compiles security intent into executable, compliance-checked network topologies. It uses a schema contract to constrain generation, retrieves reference architectures from a curated template library via dense-vector search, and applies staged fusion for intent-template alignment and security completion. The generated topology is checked against CIS Controls v8.1.2 safeguards visible at the topology layer, while unresolved cases are marked for manual review. Structural gaps are repaired through additive schema-preserving edits. The final topology is exported to Mininet scripts with kernel-level iptables ACLs, enabling executable reachability and allow/deny tests. Because no public benchmark exists for this requirement-to-topology task, we construct an evaluation set from reference security architecture diagrams. The retrieval set contains 22 templates and 44 synthetic intents across five scenarios, while the held-out set contains 7 templates and 14 intents from finance and government scenarios excluded from retrieval. On the held-out set, additive repair improves topology-visible CIS satisfaction from 0.78 to 1.00 in fewer than 1.5 rounds on average, and one feedback round raises the post-ACL policy pass rate from 0.78 to 0.88.

</details>


### [185] [Agreement Is Not Alignment: Divergent Moral Grounds in Human and LLM Ethical Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12368)
*Octavian M. Machidon,Alina L. Machidon,Vojko Strahovnik,Mateja Centa Strahovnik,Jonas Miklavčič,Marko Robnik Šikonja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文基于500个ETHICS衍生基准条目，对比人类标注者与LLM在最终标签和道德理由两个层面的一致性，发现模型虽在标签层面与人类高度一致，但在道德理由层面存在系统性分歧，表明"同意"不等于"对齐"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常用与人类判断的一致性来评估LLM的道德对齐，但标签一致并不能证明模型与人类基于相同的道德理由、原则或情境解释做出判断。这种"表面一致"可能掩盖深层的推理分歧。

Method: 构建包含500个条目的ETHICS衍生基准，覆盖五个道德判断领域；收集人类标注者和多个LLM（前沿及开源模型）的最终标签与支持性理由，进行标签层面的一致性比较和理由层面的内容分析。

Result: 模型与人类标注者多数投票在最终标签上经常高度一致，但理由层面的分析揭示系统性的道德理由分歧。模型在伤害、尊重、守诺、公正、应得和借口相关性等类别的注意力分配上不同于人类，即使最终标签一致时也是如此。

Conclusion: 标签一致性不应被视为道德对齐的等价物。仅基于标签的评估可能产生误导性的安全感，必须辅以对模型判断中表达的理由、原则和道德优先级的分析。

Abstract: Agreement with human judgments is a common proxy for evaluating the alignment of large language models (LLMs). Yet agreement in final labels does not show that human annotators and models rely on the same moral grounds. Two agents may reach the same judgment while appealing to different principles, contextual assumptions, or interpretations of the situation.
  We test this distinction using a curated 500-item ETHICS-derived benchmark spanning five domains of moral judgment, with new human annotator and LLM annotations of both final labels and supporting rationales. Across frontier and open model families, agreement with human annotator majority labels is often high. However, rationale-level analysis reveals systematic divergence in the moral grounds expressed by human annotators and models. In particular, models redistribute attention across categories such as harm, respect, promise-keeping, justice, desert, and excuse relevance, even when their final labels match the human annotator majority.
  Our results show that agreement should not be treated as equivalent to alignment. Label-based evaluation can therefore be misleadingly reassuring unless complemented by analysis of the reasons, principles, and moral priorities expressed in model judgments.

</details>


### [186] [Multi-Agent Scheduling with LLM-Assisted Contract Net Negotiation for Stream Processing in Mobile Edge Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12371)
*Sabeur Lajili,Zaki Brahmi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MAS-DecStream，一种面向移动边缘-云异构基础设施的流处理调度系统，其核心贡献是LLM-MR-CNP：将经典Contract Net Protocol扩展为语义CFP、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆和确定性验证。实验基于阿里ASI Trace，在三级别上评估，减少延迟违规至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用较基线提升22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流处理系统越来越多地运行在异构移动边缘-云基础设施上，面临工作负载波动、资源争用和严格的QoS要求，这使得去中心化调度变得复杂，传统方法难以应对这些挑战。

Method: 提出MAS-DecStream系统，核心是LLM-MR-CNP协议，包括：语义化的CFP（调用方案征集）表述、渐进式上下文披露、多轮提案修订、协商记忆以及确定性验证。边缘集群代理结合局部观测、预测资源状态和定性运行时上下文，用自然语言细化卸载提案，同时硬性资源与QoS约束保持确定性。

Result: 实验基于阿里ASI Trace，从三个层面评估：单轮vs多轮CNP、规则式vs LLM辅助细化、固定模型单轮vs多轮协商。结果显示延迟违规降至3%，消除资源过度承诺，20个代理下冲突解决率达0.91，效用较多轮规则基线提升22%。另外25案例评估显示模型和提示词相关的精度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: 多轮CNP细化是协议层面的主要收益来源，LLM辅助在处理定性和不确定的运行时上下文时增加额外价值，为异构边缘-云环境下的去中心化流处理调度提供了初步证据支持。

Abstract: Stream-processing systems increasingly operate across heterogeneous mobile edge--cloud infrastructures, where workload volatility, resource contention, and stringent quality-of-service (QoS) requirements complicate decentralized scheduling. This paper proposes \emph{MAS-DecStream}, whose main contribution is \emph{LLM-MR-CNP}: an extension of the classical Contract Net Protocol with semantic CFP formulation, progressive context disclosure, multi-round proposal revision, negotiation memory, and deterministic validation. Edge-cluster agents refine natural-language offloading proposals from local observations, predicted resource states, and qualitative runtime context, while hard resource and QoS constraints remain deterministic. Experiments derived from the Alibaba ASI Trace evaluate the extension at three levels: single- versus multi-round CNP, rule-based versus LLM-assisted refinement, and fixed-model single- versus multi-round negotiation. Under the evaluated configurations, MAS-DecStream reduces latency violations to 3\%, eliminates resource overcommitment, reaches a conflict-resolution rate of 0.91 with 20 agents, and improves utility by up to 22\% over the multi-round rule-based baseline. A separate 25-case evaluation shows model- and prompt-dependent accuracy--cost trade-offs. The results provide initial evidence that multi-round CNP refinement is the principal protocol-level gain, with LLM assistance adding value for qualitative and uncertain runtime context.

</details>


### [187] [Position: We Need Practical AI Alignment Methods to Mirror Human Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12372)
*Vijay Keswani,Breanna K. Nguyen,Cyrus Cousins,Vincent Conitzer,Walter Sinnott-Armstrong,Jana Schaich Borg*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"认知对齐"（cognitive alignment）概念，主张在高风险决策场景中，AI系统不仅需要行为正确，还应以与用户相似的推理方式进行思考，并忠实地传达其推理逻辑。文章回顾了认知对齐提升可理解性和可信度的证据，通过新调查数据表明许多用户认为认知对齐"必不可少"，同时指出现有对齐方法的不足并提出研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI从决策辅助逐步演变为决策委托甚至自主决策者，用户对AI推理过程的理解和信任变得至关重要。然而，现有的对齐方法主要关注行为对齐（让AI输出正确结果），忽略了思维过程的对齐，这可能导致用户即使面对正确结果也无法理解或信任AI，从而阻碍AI在高风险场景中的采纳。

Method: 本文是立场论文（position paper），采用综述与实证相结合的方法：1）系统回顾认知对齐对可理解性和可信度影响的已有证据；2）开展用户调查收集新数据，评估用户对认知对齐重要性的态度；3）分析现有对齐方法与认知对齐目标之间的差距；4）提出面向认知对齐的未来研究议程。

Result: 调查数据显示相当比例的用户认为当AI的判断或行动理由对自己重要时，认知对齐是"必不可少"的。文献综述支持认知对齐能提升系统的可理解性和可信度。同时发现现有对齐方法（如RLHF、行为克隆等）主要聚焦行为层面，与认知对齐所需的推理层面存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 认知错位（cognitive misalignment）是AI在许多设想应用中落地采用的潜在障碍。实现认知对齐对创建用户既愿意依赖也有理由依赖的AI系统至关重要，需要学术界针对推理层面的对齐展开系统性研究。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly employed as decision aids, decision delegates, or autonomous decision-makers. This position paper argues that in many settings, particularly high-stakes decision-making, we need accurate cognitively-aligned AI systems that reason similarly to their users, and faithfully communicate their reasoning. We review evidence that cognitive alignment improves understandability and trustworthiness, and provide new survey data showing that many users find cognitive alignment "essential" when an AI's rationale for a judgment or action is important to them. We outline the gaps between existing alignment methods and what is needed to achieve cognitive alignment, and present a research agenda to address these gaps. We argue that cognitive misalignment represents a likely impediment to AI adoption in many envisioned applications, and that addressing it is important for creating AI systems on which users are both willing and justified to rely.

</details>


### [188] [Don't Want Your LLM to Recommend Nuclear Strike? Try Asking It in Japanese](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12373)
*Rian Touchent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过高风险的博弈论场景测试，发现大语言模型的安全行为是语言依赖的：当模型被要求用日语推理时，Claude和Gemini等模型的"发动核打击"率显著下降，而用英语推理则保持较高打击率。机制研究发现，是"推理语言"而非"输入语言"驱动了这一效应。仅用英语评估LLM安全对齐会遗漏其他语言中隐含的风险与防护措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地用于战略和咨询场景，但其安全对齐通常仅用英语评估。研究者质疑：语言本身是否会改变模型在高风险决策中的行为？现有安全评估忽略了语言维度的潜在影响。

Method: 使用单轮博弈论小场景（vignettes），让模型为核武国家提供是否打击无助对手的建议。提示词在战略上跨语言完全相同且刻意非道德化。测试6家供应商的9个模型，并设计跨语言分离实验：在英文提示中指令模型用日语推理，以隔离输入语言与推理语言的影响。

Result: 日语提示显著降低Claude家族和Gemini Pro 3.1的打击率（Claude Sonnet 4.6从40%降至0%，从93%降至17%；Gemini从53%降至13%），但策略上合理时影响最小。跨语言实验证实：用日语推理（而非日语输入）是驱动因素，且日语推理时模型自发产生提示中完全不含的道德词汇。另外5个模型无语言效应，但在所有条件下几乎都选择打击。

Conclusion: LLM安全行为是语言依赖的，仅用英语评估会同时遗漏其他语言中隐藏的风险和安全保障。语言效应依赖于模型在英语中已存在犹豫的基础能力。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in strategic and advisory contexts, yet their safety alignment is typically evaluated in English only. We test nine models from six providers and ask whether the language of a prompt can change a model's decision in a high-stakes scenario. We use single-turn game-theoretic vignettes in which a model advises a nuclear-armed nation on whether to strike a defenseless opponent. The prompt is intentionally amoral and strategically identical across languages. We find that Japanese prompts reduce launch rates in the Claude model family: Claude Sonnet 4.6 drops from 40% to 0% in scenarios where the strike is unnecessary and from 93% to 17% in contested scenarios, with minimal effect when the strike is strategically rational. The effect extends to Gemini Pro 3.1 (53% to 13%). A cross-language experiment isolates the mechanism: when instructed to reason in Japanese in an English prompt, launch rates drop from 93% to 37%. It is the language the model is asked to reason in, not the language of the input, that drives the effect. When reasoning in Japanese, models spontaneously generate moral vocabulary (''moral cost'', ''millions of lives'') that is entirely absent from the prompt. Five other models show no language effect, but they launch in nearly every condition regardless of language. The effect requires a model that already hesitates in English. These results show that LLM safety behavior is language-dependent, and that evaluating in English alone can miss both risks and safeguards encoded in other languages.

</details>


### [189] [Dual-Flow Transformers: Decoupling the Primary Prefill Path from Additional Decode Computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12385)
*Liming Liu,Mingze Wang,Tuo Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 这篇论文提出了Dual-Flow Transformer架构，通过在主语言模型流之外增加一个辅助流（仅在解码阶段激活），将额外的计算量分配到续写预测（decode）阶段，而不增加提示处理（prefill）阶段的成本，从而实现推理阶段计算资源的阶段特异性分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM服务请求增多，累积推理成本相对一次性训练成本越来越重要。传统宽度或深度扩展会同时增加prefill和decode两个阶段的成本，因为每一层在两个阶段都被计算。作者希望将额外的学习计算量分配到续写预测（decode）阶段，同时保留提示范围的初级计算和单一持久KV缓存，以更高效地利用推理资源。

Method: Dual-Flow Transformer包含两条流：主流是完整的因果语言模型，处理提示并写入KV缓存；辅助流在提示处理阶段被省略，仅从最终提示位置开始激活，增加续写预测计算但不写入持久状态也不影响主流。两条流共享主要的注意力、MLP和输出矩阵，但使用独立的token嵌入和轻量耦合。在MoE模型中，主辅助专家扇出是独立的控制变量，可分别控制prompt成本、续写成本和预测质量。

Result: 在匹配token数（matched-token）比较中，Dual-Flow在多种架构和数据配置下取得了更低的验证损失。在MoE模型中研究了两个机制：固定prefill专家计算增加decode计算，以及在固定decode专家预算下在两条流之间重新分配。实验揭示了prefill-decode质量权衡，并展示了相位特异性专家分配的潜力。

Conclusion: Dual-Flow Transformer证明了可以通过阶段特异性（phase-specific）的计算分配来解耦prefill和decode的成本与质量，为推理阶段的计算资源优化提供了新的设计方向，特别是在MoE架构中具有灵活控制推理成本的潜力。

Abstract: As large language models serve more requests, cumulative inference cost is becoming increasingly important relative to one-time training cost. The two inference phases stress hardware differently: prompt prefill is parallel and typically compute-bound, whereas autoregressive decode is sequential and often memory-bandwidth-bound. Conventional width or depth scaling increases both costs together because every added layer is evaluated in both phases. We ask whether additional learned computation can instead be allocated to continuation prediction while preserving the prompt-wide primary computation and a single persistent key-value (KV) cache. We introduce the Dual-Flow Transformer. Its primary flow is a complete causal language model that processes the prompt and writes the KV cache. The auxiliary flow is omitted during prompt processing and activated only from the final prompt position onward, adding continuation-prediction computation without writing persistent state or influencing the primary flow. The two flows share major attention, MLP, and output matrices, while using separate token embeddings and lightweight coupling. Sharing weights and the primary cache also creates opportunities to reuse loaded weights and cached keys and values during grouped execution. Across matched-token comparisons, Dual-Flow achieves lower validation loss across architectures and data configurations. In MoE models, the separation makes primary and auxiliary expert fan-outs independent controls over prompt cost, continuation cost, and predictive quality. We study two regimes: increasing decode computation at fixed prefill expert computation, and reallocating a fixed decode expert budget between the two flows. These experiments expose a prefill-decode-quality trade-off and demonstrate the potential of phase-specific expert allocation.

</details>


### [190] [Learning to Adapt Cross-Domain Preferences via Meta-LoRA for LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12389)
*Xuefei Wang,Jun Han,Zixuan Wang,Qingkai Zeng,Xiao Wang,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA方法，用于跨域零样本或少样本个性化对话生成。方法通过元学习的LoRA初始化作为适应起点和先验中心，根据支持集规模和预测不确定性动态调整更新强度，同时将个性化先验分解为用户和域两个组件（人类可读提示+拓扑保持软token），在HiCUPID等基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域个性化方法存在两个核心问题：(1) 适应方法难以在稀疏证据下校准更新幅度，容易过拟合；(2) 历史迁移方法常将用户偏好与源域伪影纠缠在一起，导致不可靠的个性化先验和负迁移。

Method: 1) PAC-Bayes正则化的Meta-LoRA：以元学习的LoRA初始化作为适应起点和先验中心，根据支持集大小和预测不确定性调整更新强度，在稀疏证据下限制过拟合，证据充足时允许更强个性化；2) 个性化先验功能分解：将先验分为用户组件（人类可读提示，表达稳定偏好）和域组件（拓扑保持的软token，用于域特定隐空间条件化）。

Result: 在多个基准和个性化任务上持续优于强基线。在HiCUPID上，跨域胜率退化相对于最佳竞争基线降低47.9%，在未见用户冷启动场景下胜率提升110.2%。

Conclusion: PAC-Bayes正则化Meta-LoRA通过证据质量校准适应强度和先验功能分解，有效解决了跨域个性化中的过拟合和负迁移问题，显著提升了冷启动和稀疏数据场景下的个性化对话生成性能。

Abstract: Cross-domain zero- or few-shot personalization aims to generate user-preferred responses in unseen conversational domains from only a handful of target-domain interactions. Existing adaptation methods struggle to calibrate update magnitude under sparse evidence and thus overfit, whereas history-transfer methods often entangle user preferences with source-domain artifacts, yielding unreliable personalization priors and negative transfer. To calibrate adaptation to evidence quality, we propose PAC-Bayes-regularized Meta-LoRA, which uses a meta-learned LoRA initialization as both the adaptation start and prior center, while adjusting update strength according to support-set size and predictive uncertainty. This limits overfitting under sparse or ambiguous evidence while permitting stronger personalization as evidence grows. Controlled adaptation alone does not determine which preferences should transfer across domains or how they should be expressed. We therefore functionally decompose personalization priors into user and domain components, using a human-readable prompt for stable preferences and topology-preserving soft tokens for domain-specific hidden-space conditioning. Experiments across multiple benchmarks and personalization tasks show consistent gains over strong baselines. On HiCUPID, our method reduces cross-domain win-rate degradation by 47.9% relative to the best competing baseline and improves win rate by 110.2% under unseen-user cold start.

</details>


### [191] [Research Assistant: AstraZeneca's Agentic System for R&D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12395)
*Piotr Grabowski,Mohamed Alameen,Jorge Bretones,Sabina Cardell,Miguel Carmona,Gavin Edwards,Ben Grainger,Sameh Hassan,Erik Jansson,Artur Kuziakhmetov,Albert Maristany,Hebatallah Mohamed,Andriy Nikolov,Sebastian Nilsson,Mark O'Donoghue,James Pacileo,Ashiq Sultan,Alex Voegele,Michael Ughetto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个由阿斯利康内部开发的基于大语言模型的研究助手系统（Research Assistant），该系统通过聊天式界面整合科学文献、知识图谱、化学、临床试验、安全资源、表达数据及内部实验系统等多种数据源，支持快速问答和复杂多步研究任务，响应结果基于检索证据并链接回原始来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助科学家和临床研究人员跨多种异构数据源高效探索生物医学问题，支持日常研发工作流，提升研究效率。

Method: 构建一个LLM为基础的系统，提供聊天式界面，整合多类数据源；支持快速模式（直接问答）和多步模式（复杂研究任务）；响应基于检索证据，并链接回原始数据源。

Result: 系统已在大规模部署，支持阿斯利康日常研发工作流；本文阐述了系统架构、产品设计选择和部署经验教训。

Conclusion: 通过整合多种数据源并提供可追溯的证据链接，该系统能够有效支持生物医学研究中的日常问答和复杂研究任务。

Abstract: We describe Research Assistant, an internal LLM-based system developed at AstraZeneca to help scientists and clinicians explore biomedical questions across a broad range of data sources. The system provides a chat-style interface that brings together evidence from scientific literature, knowledge graphs, chemistry, clinical trials, safety resources, expression data, and internal experimental systems. It supports both a fast mode for direct question answering and a multi-step mode for more complex research tasks. Responses are grounded in retrieved evidence and linked back to the original sources, allowing users to review and further explore the underlying data. In this technical note, we outline the system architecture, the main design choices behind the product, and lessons learned from deploying it at scale to support day-to-day R&D workflows across AstraZeneca.

</details>


### [192] [Large Language Models Can Follow Instructions, But Not Many at Once: Phase Transitions in Compositional Constraint Satisfaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12426)
*Mariya I. Vasileva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出约束饱和评估（CSE）基准，系统研究大语言模型在多重约束组合下的退化现象，发现随约束数量增加，逐约束通过率缓慢下降但全部满足的概率急剧崩塌，且不同约束类型退化速度不同，失败近乎独立导致性能呈乘法式累积衰减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已能熟练处理单一约束，但现实中常需同时满足多个约束（推理结构、安全边界、输出格式等）。组合约束场景的性能退化机制尚不清楚：退化速度如何、由什么决定、能否缓解，亟需系统研究。

Method: 构建程序化生成的CSE基准，系统改变同时约束数量k（1-12），每个约束由确定性规则验证器评分，完全无需LLM评判。覆盖15个模型、36种约束类型，共369,753次检查。通过分析逐约束通过率、全约束满足率及不同约束类型的退化差异来刻画组合退化规律。

Result: 三个核心发现：(1)逐约束通过率平缓可预测地下降，但全部满足k个约束的概率急剧崩塌——单约束通过率约41%的模型在k=8时全部满足仅5.7%；(2)结构约束比词汇约束每个额外约束多损失2倍基线能力，退化差异可通过"理解-维持"间隙解释，区分需持续追踪的约束与免疫组合的二元决策；(3)失败近乎独立导致累积呈乘法效应，残余耦合与共享输出特征相关而非成对干扰。可靠遵循指令在5-6个约束后崩溃：最强模型在7个约束时探针级成功率低于50%，15个模型中有12个在3个或更少约束时即失败。

Conclusion: 同时指令遵循在超出约5-6个约束后系统性失效，且多数模型在极少的约束数量下即崩溃。研究揭示了组合退化的乘法累积本质、约束类型异质退化机制及失败独立性假设的近似有效性，为理解和改善LLM多约束指令遵循能力提供了基准与理论洞见。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed in settings that require simultaneous adherence to multiple explicit constraints - reasoning structure, safety boundaries, output schemas. Individual constraints are handled proficiently, but the compositional regime, where many must hold jointly, remains poorly characterized: how rapidly does performance degrade, what governs the degradation, and can the collapse be mitigated? We introduce Constraint Saturation Evaluation (CSE), a procedurally generated benchmark that systematically varies the number of simultaneous constraints (k), with every constraint scored by a deterministic, rule-based verifier and zero LLM-judge involvement: 15 models, 36 constraint types, 369,753 checks at k=1-12. Three findings emerge. First, per-constraint pass rate decays gradually and predictably, while the chance of satisfying all k constraints collapses - a model passing individual constraints at ~41% at k=8 succeeds on all eight just 5.7% of the time. Second, constraints do not degrade equally: structural constraints lose 2x more baseline capability per added constraint than lexical ones, ordered by a comprehension-maintenance gap that separates constraints requiring sustained tracking from binary decisions immune to composition. Third, failures are nearly independent, which is what makes the accumulation multiplicative; the residual coupling that does exist tracks shared output features rather than pairwise interference - a wrong sentence count fails every constraint that reads it. Reliable instruction following breaks down beyond 5-6 simultaneous constraints: probe-level success falls below 50% at 7 constraints for the strongest model, and at 3 or fewer for 12 of 15.

</details>


### [193] [MindMemOS: A Portable and Self-Evolving Memory Operating Layer for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12428)
*Kaichao Liang,Yuqi Cui,Hao Kong,Xinyuan Huang,Guohaotian Hou,Qingcan Kang,Liang Chen,Yiyang Yin,Ke Ye,Jiaquan Guo,Da Chen,Lingan Zeng,Yixing Peng,Rong Yao,Shixiong Kai,Mingxuan Yuan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了MindMemOS，一个可移植且自我进化的记忆操作系统层，通过统一的实体属性时间结构组织开放世界信息，并配套MindMemEvolve（基于验证驱动的进化搜索优化记忆模式）、dreaming（合并冗余记录并解决冲突）以及MindSkillEvolve（将执行轨迹转化为可复用技能）等算法，实现记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识的持续自适应进化。实验在LOCOMO上达到94.03%准确率，PersonaMem上70.63%，MindSkillEvolve将SpreadsheetBench成功率较初始技能基线提升9.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI智能体的记忆系统在开发完成后通常保持固定不变，无法根据持续使用适应其记忆模型、组织策略和程序性知识，限制了智能体在长期交互中的学习与自适应能力。

Method: 提出了MindMemOS记忆操作系统层，核心包括：(1) 统一的实体属性时间（entity property time）结构组织开放世界信息；(2) MindMemEvolve算法，利用验证驱动的进化搜索为特定场景优化记忆模式；(3) dreaming机制，通过合并冗余记录和解决冲突来巩固累积记忆；(4) 隐式纠正反馈作为人在回路信号修正不准确或不对齐的记忆；(5) MindSkillEvolve算法，将智能体执行轨迹转化为可复用且逐步精炼的技能。

Result: MindMemOS在LOCOMO基准上达到94.03%准确率，在PersonaMem上达到70.63%。MindSkillEvolve在SpreadsheetBench上较初始技能基线提升9.2个百分点的成功率。

Conclusion: MindMemOS展示了记忆系统自我进化的可行性，通过动态调整记忆模式、整合记忆冲突及从执行轨迹中提炼技能，有效提升了AI智能体的长期记忆能力与任务执行性能。

Abstract: Memory is a core component of AI agents, enabling them to accumulate experience, maintain personalization, and adapt over long-term interactions. However, existing memory systems often remain fixed after development, limiting their ability to adapt their memory models, organization strategies, and procedural knowledge through continued use. We present MindMemOS, a portable and self-evolving memory operating layer that organizes open-world information using a unified entity property timestructure. MindMemOS supports scenario-adaptive memory modeling, higher-order pattern discovery, autonomous memory refinement, and continuous skill evolution. Its MindMemEvolve algorithm employs validation-driven evolutionary search to optimize memory schemas for target scenarios, whiledreaming consolidates accumulated memories by merging redundant records and resolving conflicts. In addition, implicit corrective feedback serves as a human-in-the-loop signal for identifying and revising potentially inaccurate or misaligned memories. Its MindSkillEvolve algorithm further transforms agent execution trajectories into reusable and progressively refined skills. MindMemOS achieves 94.03% accuracy on LOCOMO and 70.63% on PersonaMem. MindSkillEvolve improves SpreadsheetBench success by 9.2 percentage points over the initial-skill baseline.

</details>


### [194] [Governed Persistent Memory: Source-Bound State Semantics and Fail-Closed Release for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12476)
*Guodong Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出治理型持久记忆（GPM），一个可审计的双时态状态转换模型，用于解决长期智能体记忆中检索结果可能包含矛盾、过时、已撤销或已删除记录的问题。GPM通过源绑定准入、派生生命周期状态、当前公共屏障和故障关闭的结构化释放来确保输出声明的完整性。在GPM-ReleaseBench基准、端到端服务评估、有限模型验证和差分测试中均展现出高可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统长期智能体记忆被简化为"选择—存储—检索"流水线，但检索过程并未裁决矛盾、被取代、被撤销、已删除或过时的记录是否支持即将输出的声明，可能导致智能体基于无效记忆产生不可靠输出。

Method: 引入治理型持久记忆（GPM），一种可审计的双时态状态转换模型，包含：源绑定准入机制、派生生命周期状态、当前公共屏障、故障关闭的结构化释放，以及五条可执行条款（账本完整性、源绑定、冲突隔离、撤销/删除后禁止复活、在已验证头部的全新视图上的精确声明闭合）。

Result: 在GPM-ReleaseBench的3,600个案例中完全匹配所有结果；对比策略仅匹配1,800/3,600并在50%违规案例上产生未匹配释放。密封端到端评估V3臂中治理通道2,400/2,400正确，未治理基线仅600/2,400，且修复全部1,800个基线失败零回归。V5重新密封（中英文指令臂）同样达到每臂2,400/2,400。有限模型探索331,776个语义状态和1,990,656个查询状态无反例，100,000条追踪三引擎差分测试零不匹配。

Conclusion: GPM提供的是有界契约和实现层面的保证，而非开放世界模型精度或世界真理的证据。结果显示治理架构能显著提升长期记忆检索输出的可靠性，但不应过度解读为语言模型本身达到完美准确。

Abstract: Long-term agent memory is usually treated as select--store--retrieve, but retrieval does not decide whether contradictory, superseded, retracted, deleted, or stale records may support an outgoing claim. We introduce Governed Persistent Memory (GPM), an auditable bitemporal state-transition model with source-bound admission, derived lifecycle state, current public barriers, and fail-closed structured release. Five executable clauses cover ledger integrity, source binding, conflict isolation, non-revival after retraction or deletion, and exact claim closure over a fresh view at one verified head.
  On a prespecified hash-frozen 3,600-case GPM-ReleaseBench, GPM matches all complete outcomes; the strongest of three intentionally simple complete policies matches 1,800/3,600 and makes unmatched releases on 50% of violation cases. A separate sealed end-to-end service evaluation exercises real ingestion and release across eight query families. In its publicly disclosed V3 arm, the governed lane is correct on 2,400/2,400 clusters versus 600/2,400 for ungoverned local Qwen2.5-7B; it repairs all 1,800 baseline failures with no regression (one-sided 95% lower bounds 99.875% and 99.834%). A later V5 reseal over Chinese- and English-command arms, with generation-date pinning and no post-freeze reducer amendment, again obtains 2,400/2,400 per arm. A production-code-independent finite model explores 331,776 semantic and 1,990,656 query states without a full-contract counterexample, and a 100,000-trace three-engine differential yields zero mismatches.
  These are bounded contract and implementation results, not open-world model accuracy or evidence of world truth. Governed answers in the sealed service evaluation are deterministic service outputs; the 7B result is the ungoverned comparison, not a claim that a language model itself became perfectly accurate.

</details>


### [195] [$\varepsilon$-MemEvo: Adaptive Cross-Task Memory Transfer for LLM Program Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12522)
*Aofan Liu,Shiyuan Song,Yiyan Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ε-MemEvo是一个用于LLM程序进化的跨任务知识迁移框架，它将先验经验存储为任务无关的策略性记忆（而非原始代码），并通过自适应注入门控制记忆的注入与否及强度，避免负迁移。在8个基准任务上相对AdaEvolve平均提升+8.7%的AUCC，且计算开销小于1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM程序进化系统（如FunSearch、AlphaEvolve）虽然能发现新颖算法，但每个任务都是孤立优化的，搜索完成后经验即被丢弃，无法实现跨任务的知识复用。

Method: 1) 将先验经验存储为任务无关的紧凑自然语言策略记忆（而非原始代码），支持跨不同API和评估器的迁移；2) 设计自适应注入门，根据检索到的记忆与当前任务的语义匹配度，动态决定是否注入以及注入强度；3) 采用内容级留一协议（Leave-One-Out）进行评估，排除目标任务的记忆条目。

Result: 在8个覆盖数学优化和系统工程领域的基准上，基于GPT-5主骨干，ε-MemEvo在所有8个任务上均优于AdaEvolve，平均相对增益+8.7%，早期收敛速度平均提升+9.4%。消融实验表明朴素记忆注入可能灾难性失败，而自适应门控在所有5个消融任务中保持安全。后验数据在观测状态下可解释：改进搜索期偏向跳过，早期与晚期平台期从跳过转向提示。计算开销小于1%。

Conclusion: ε-MemEvo证明了通过任务无关的策略记忆和自适应门控，可以安全高效地实现LLM程序进化中的跨任务知识迁移，同时保持可解释性和极低的计算开销。

Abstract: LLM-based program evolution systems such as FunSearch and AlphaEvolve have shown strong ability to discover novel algorithms, but typically optimize each task in isolation, discarding search experience after completion. We introduce $\varepsilon$-MemEvo, a framework for cross-task knowledge transfer in LLM program evolution. $\varepsilon$-MemEvo stores prior experience as task-agnostic tactic memories: compact natural-language summaries of successful algorithmic strategies rather than raw code, enabling transfer across tasks with different APIs and evaluators. To avoid negative transfer from semantically mismatched memories, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo uses an adaptive injection gate that decides whether retrieved memories should be injected, and at what intensity. We evaluate $\varepsilon$-MemEvo on 8 diverse optimization benchmarks spanning mathematical optimization and systems engineering, using a content-level Leave-One-Out protocol that excludes target-task memory entries. On the primary GPT-5 backbone, $\varepsilon$-MemEvo improves AUCC over AdaEvolve on all 8 tasks, with a mean relative gain of +8.7%, and improves early-stage convergence by +9.4% on average. Ablations show that naive memory injection can fail catastrophically, while adaptive gating remains safe across all five ablation tasks. The data-updated posterior is interpretable in observed states: it favors skip during improving search and shifts from skip to hint across early and late plateaus. These gains incur less than 1% computational overhead.

</details>


### [196] [QuoteBench: How Matched Scores Can Hide Command-Path Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13547)
*Shangao Li,Yao Zhang,Volker Tresp,Yuanyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出QuoteBench基准，通过56个单次任务和14个事故衍生家族，测量LLM编码智能体在命令生成与执行传输之间的边界。该基准通过添加一个故意不转义的解析器并披露该边界，区分命令生成错误与生成后引入的失败。实验发现，通过披露边界可恢复30.4至60.7个百分点的成功率，且原始生成能力已接近饱和，边界适应能力才是区分模型的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有匹配执行分数无法区分命令生成错误与生成后执行阶段引入的失败。LLM编码智能体通过可能序列化、包装和重新解析模型输出的接口发出Bash命令，这种双层错误混淆使得评估不准确，因此需要一种能够精确测量生成与执行边界的方法。

Method: 提出QuoteBench基准，包含56个来自14个事故衍生家族的单次任务，通过添加一个故意未转义的解析器来跨越生成契约与执行传输的边界。采用精确最终状态验证，通过对比同一回复在有无附加解析器情况下的表现，以及在披露边界前后的恢复情况，来量化生成错误与执行失败。

Result: 在八种同窗口配置中，重复播放相同回复通过附加解析器使成功率降低55.4至73.2个百分点；披露边界后，六种配置恢复30.4至60.7个百分点，另外两种配置恢复为零或轻微负值。原始生成能力在前沿已接近饱和，边界适应能力仍是区分模型的关键。GPT-5.6-sol的匹配差距-3.6点隐藏了-64.3点的损害与+60.7点的补偿。部署配置会重排模型排名：26对可比模型中有一对明确反转，四对处于单任务边缘。

Conclusion: 对命令发出智能体的评估应报告模型配置、生成契约、执行路径、操作点和最终状态验证器，而非将匹配分数视为模型的内在属性。匹配分数掩盖了生成错误与执行失败之间的关键差异，可能导致误导性的模型排名。

Abstract: LLM coding agents issue Bash commands through interfaces that may serialize, wrap, and reparse model output. Matched execution scores alone cannot distinguish command-generation errors from failures introduced after generation. QuoteBench measures this boundary with exact final-state validation on 56 one-shot tasks from 14 incident-derived families, crossing the generation contract with the execution transport around one deliberately unescaped added parser. Escaping at the interpolation point reproduces each replayed reply's raw-path outcome, so any recovery under a disclosed boundary must come from the model changing its generation. Across eight same-window configurations, replaying the same reply through the added parser lowers success by 55.4 to 73.2 percentage points; disclosure recovers 30.4 to 60.7 points for six configurations, and zero or slightly negative for the other two. Raw generation is nearly saturated at the frontier; boundary adaptation is what still separates models. GPT-5.6-sol's matched gap of -3.6 points hides -64.3 points of damage and +60.7 points of compensation. The deployment configuration reorders models: one reversal among 26 comparable pairs is unambiguous and four more sit on single-task margins. Evaluations of command-issuing agents should report the model configuration, generation contract, execution path, operating point, and final-state validator rather than treat a matched score as an intrinsic model property.

</details>


### [197] [CAS: A Causal Attribution Score for Local and Global Explainable Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12555)
*Michael Georgiades,Charalambia Varnava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Causal Attribution Score (CAS)架构，将预测性解释方法扩展为因果解释方法，通过从识别的干预联盟博弈出发，用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，生成局部和全局的因果归因评分。核心创新在于构建了一个从局部到全局的因果报告层，明确区分"什么预测结果"与"什么解释因果效应的异质性"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的预测性解释方法（如SHAP）只能解释模型输出，无法归因干预对现实世界结果的影响效应。因果解释需要区分预测特征与治疗效应修饰因子，因此需要一个显式以干预目标为导向的因果归因框架。

Method: 基于识别出的干预联盟博弈（interventional coalition game），使用因果Shapley贡献分配联合干预对比，构建四个评分：Local CAS、Signed Local CAS以及两个互补的Global CAS摘要。并非提出新的Shapley公式，而是构建一个带显式干预目标的局部到全局因果报告层。

Result: 在已知真值基准中，8次重复主效应交互模拟（每次n=2,200，三个行动）中，联盟感知CAS的局部MAE为0.107，优于单变量归一化（0.173）和全局归一化绝对ATE向量（0.213）。在强交互下优势从可加性下的-0.003增至0.091。在两个实证DoubleML数据集上，预测性SHAP/TreeSHAP排名与Feature-CAS的治疗效应修饰因子排名存在显著差异，如Pennsylvania数据集中dep1从预测全局排名13跃升至Feature-CAS排名2。

Conclusion: CAS有效分离了"什么预测结果"与"什么解释因果效应异质性"的差异，为因果解释提供了超越预测解释的增量价值，尤其在存在交互效应和需要识别治疗效应修饰因子的场景中表现突出。

Abstract: Predictive explanation methods attribute a model output; they do not, by themselves, attribute an intervention effect on the real-world outcome. We introduce the Causal Attribution Score (CAS), a compact score architecture for causal explanation. CAS starts from an identified interventional coalition game, allocates the joint intervention contrast with causal Shapley contributions, and converts those raw outcome-scale effects into Local CAS, Signed Local CAS, and two complementary Global CAS summaries. The innovation is not a new Shapley formula, but a local-to-global causal reporting layer with an explicit intervention target. In the known-truth benchmark, eight repeated primary-interaction simulations (n = 2,200 each, three actions) gave mean Local CAS MAE of 0.107 for coalition-aware CAS, compared with 0.173 for one-at-a-time normalisation and 0.213 for a global normalised absolute ATE vector. The paired advantage over one-at-a-time normalisation increased from -0.003 under additivity to 0.091 under strong interactions. On both empirical DoubleML datasets, 401(k) eligibility/net financial assets (n = 9,915) and Pennsylvania reemployment bonus/unemployment duration (n = 5,099), predictive SHAP/TreeSHAP rankings differed materially from Feature-CAS rankings of treatment-effect modifiers. In Pennsylvania, dep1 (exactly one dependent) moved from predictive global rank 13 to Feature-CAS rank 2 and was the leading local Feature-CAS modifier. These results isolate the added value of separating what predicts the outcome from what explains heterogeneity in an estimated causal effect.

</details>


### [198] [Trie Automata for Constrained Decoding over Large Finite Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12574)
*Xingzi Xu,Karim Bouyarmane*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"trie automaton"的专用解码机制，利用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配算法预计算每个节点的token掩码，解决了大规模语言模型在有限集合约束下的结构化输出解码性能瓶颈（"cardinality wall"问题），相比XGrammar实现了29倍的端到端吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要生成符合预定义模式的结构化输出，其中常见的约束是从有限有效字符串集合中选择。当前受约束解码系统通过通用语法编译来处理，但当有效值数量增长到数千时速度变得极慢（即"cardinality wall"），因此需要一种利用有限集合结构特性的专门机制来突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 提出trie automaton机制，通过三个关键设计：(1)利用有限集合的共享前缀、有界深度和已知基数的结构特性；(2)采用Aho-Corasick多模式匹配算法预计算每个节点的token掩码；(3)预计算掩码使得无状态服务路径绕过引导解码流水线，从而在批处理服务中获得额外优势。

Result: 相比XGrammar：单步有效token计算快7倍（0.65us vs 5.8us）；K>=300时编译快2-6.5倍；端到端vLLM吞吐量在batch size=256时达到219 req/s vs 7.5 req/s（29倍）；跨七种tokenizer族（32K-262K词汇量）在K=10,000时保持亚100ms编译时间，每步成本与集合大小无关，且保证100%输出有效性。

Conclusion: 通过利用有限集合同有结构特性并预计算掩码，trie automaton在性能和效率上显著超越了基于通用语法编译的方案，其无状态服务路径的架构优势使得该机制在批处理场景下能够获得数量级的吞吐量提升，为大规模LLM结构化输出解码提供了一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to generate structured outputs that conform to predefined schemas, with one common constraint being selection from a finite set of valid strings. Current constrained decoding systems handle this through general-purpose grammar compilation, which becomes prohibitively slow as the number of valid values grows into the thousands, a cardinality wall. We introduce the trie automaton, a specialized mechanism that exploits finite-set structure (shared prefixes, bounded depth, known cardinality) via Aho-Corasick multi-pattern matching to precompute per-node token masks. The trie achieves 7X faster per-step valid-token computation (0.65 us vs. 5.8 us) compared to XGrammar, one of the primary backends in vLLM and SGLang, and 2--6.5X faster compilation at K >= 300. Because precomputed masks enable a stateless serving path that bypasses the guided decoding pipeline, this advantage compounds in batch serving: end-to-end vLLM throughput reaches 219 req/s vs. XGrammar's 7.5 req/s at batch size 256 (29X). The 29X combines the algorithmic speedup with integration-path savings that only precomputed masks can unlock. Across seven tokenizer families (32K--262K vocabulary), the trie maintains sub-100ms compilation up to K = 10,000 and flat per-step cost regardless of set size, while guaranteeing 100% output validity.

</details>


### [199] [Reasoning Jury: Multi-Model Consensus for Evaluating Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12585)
*Congchao Wang,Diwakar Singh,Qiaozi Gao,Spyros Matsoukas,Yang Liu,Mahdi Namazifar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Reasoning Jury系统，用一组开源LLM组成的"评审团"配合主持人共识机制替代单一评判模型，用于识别长推理轨迹中的推理缺陷。该系统通过多模型相互批判和投票修正的讨论过程达成共识，在识别推理缺陷上显著优于前沿封闭模型，且成本仅为后者的8-15%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进推理型LLM需要准确判断长推理轨迹的质量，但单一模型评判（即使是前沿模型）在识别推理缺陷上表现不佳。同时，在线训练推理LLM时通常被禁止使用前沿封闭模型，因此需要一种不依赖前沿模型的高保真推理质量评判方法。

Method: Reasoning Jury系统用一组开源LLM（如gpt-oss-120b）作为陪审团，替代单一评判模型。系统通过主持人（moderator）引导陪审员之间进行讨论，陪审员相互批判对方的判断并可修改初始投票，最后由主持人通过讨论达成共识或整合各评判结果来形成最终判断。

Result: Reasoning Jury（使用开源模型如gpt-oss-120b组成的陪审团）在正确识别推理缺陷方面显著超越前沿模型（opus-4.6、sonnet-4.6和gemini-3.1-pro）。陪审团的综合成本仅为使用前沿模型进行LLM-as-a-judge设定成本的8-15%。此外，这些判断还可用于深入理解推理LLM在基准测试中的失败模式。

Conclusion: 多模型评审团配合主持共识机制能够以低成本高精度地实现推理缺陷识别，且不依赖被禁止使用的前沿封闭模型，为推理数据策展、强化学习训练信号和模型性能理解提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Improving reasoning LLMs requires the ability to judge the quality of long reasoning traces for effective reasoning data curation, strong training signals during reinforcement learning, and an in-depth understanding of reasoning behaviors during model performance evaluation. Additionally, surfacing reasoning mistakes that the model makes would enable improving the model's performance at runtime through providing feedback. Due to the difficulty of this complex task on long reasoning traces, single-model judges (even frontier models) do not do well at identifying reasoning defects. Additionally, leveraging frontier models during online training of reasoning LLMs is generally prohibited due to guardrails in terms of use. In this work, we introduce Reasoning Jury, a system that replaces the single judge with a jury of LLMs and a moderated consensus mechanism, to improve the fidelity of judgments for identifying reasoning defects. In reasoning jury, defects of a reasoning trace and their severity are surfaced through a deliberation where a moderator conducts a discussion amongst the jury where the jurors critique each other's judgments and get to modify their initial votes. The moderator derives a consensus through deliberation amongst jurors or consolidation of judgements. We show that Reasoning Jury with a jury of open-weight models (e.g., gpt-oss-120b) is able to significantly outperform frontier models (opus-4.6, sonnet-4.6, and gemini-3.1-pro) at correctly identifying reasoning defects. Besides accuracy performance improvements, the aggregated cost of the jury (initial verdicts, deliberations, consolidation, etc.) is a fraction (8 to 15%) of the cost of running frontier models in LLM-as-a-judge setup. We also show how these judgements can be leveraged to understand failure modes of reasoning LLMs on benchmarks, which allows much deeper understanding of a model's performance.

</details>


### [200] [Auditable agentic AI for evidence-grounded thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12590)
*Haifan Gong,Shiyu Chen,Bodong Wang,Yuqi Wang,Shijie Wang,Guoliang You,Xinyu Xiong,Haowei Wang,Mingzhi Mao,Dexing Kong,Qinghua Liu,Wei Lou,Fei Chen,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ThyroidXAgent，一个临床医生可交互的智能体AI系统，用于甲状腺超声诊断和报告生成。该系统集成了专门的诊断工具，通过OpenThyroidDB（约30万张超声图像和24,000份配对报告）开发，并在28,458个测试病例上评估。系统在结节分割（Dice 87.21%）、良恶性分类（AUROC 0.9466）等任务上表现优异，并将医生诊断一致性从70.3%提升至86.2%，同时减少了35.9%的分割时间和27.4%的报告时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲状腺超声诊断需要病变定位、测量、风险分层和报告生成的协同工作，但现有大多数AI系统将这些任务孤立处理，且对临床审查的支持有限。作者旨在开发一个可审计、临床医生可纠正的智能体AI系统，以协调多个诊断任务并提供完整的病例级证据记录。

Method: 该方法构建了ThyroidXAgent智能体系统，核心包括：(1)协调多个专门诊断工具并将输出存储为可审计的病例级证据记录；(2)利用OpenThyroidDB多中心多任务数据集（约30万张图像和24,000份配对报告）进行开发；(3)采用证据驱动的报告组装方法（evidence-grounded assembly），优于多模态语言模型基线；(4)引入ThyClinScore，一种病灶级临床语义评分指标，用于评估报告质量。

Result: 在28,458个非重叠测试病例（含35个中心的8,721例NHC-MISD-TUS队列）上，系统实现：(1)结节分割平均Dice 87.21%；(2)良恶性分类平均AUROC 0.9466；(3)淋巴结转移预测AUROC 0.864，滤泡性/乳头状甲状腺癌分类AUROC 0.805；(4)医生分类准确率提升，报告诊断一致性从70.3%提升至86.2%；(5)分割和报告时间分别减少35.9%和27.4%。

Conclusion: 研究发现表明，可审计、临床医生可纠正的智能体AI系统能够有效支持甲状腺超声诊断和报告生成，在多个诊断任务上表现优异，同时通过提供证据记录和交互能力提升了临床实用性，为临床医生提供可靠的辅助决策支持。

Abstract: Thyroid ultrasound diagnosis requires coordinated lesion localization, measurement, risk stratification and reporting, yet most AI systems address these tasks in isolation and provide limited support for clinical review. We present ThyroidXAgent, a clinician-interactive agentic AI system that coordinates specialized diagnostic tools and stores their outputs as an auditable case-level evidence record. The system was developed using OpenThyroidDB, a multicentre, multitask resource integrating approximately 0.3 million ultrasound images and 24,000 paired reports, and was evaluated on 28,458 non-overlapping test cases, including 8,721 cases from 35 centres in the private NHC-MISD-TUS cohort. Across heterogeneous datasets, ThyroidXAgent achieved a mean Dice score of 87.21 percent for nodule segmentation and a mean AUROC of 0.9466 for benign-malignant classification. The same workflow supported lymph-node metastasis prediction and follicular versus papillary thyroid carcinoma classification, with AUROCs of 0.864 and 0.805, respectively. For report generation, evidence-grounded assembly outperformed multimodal language-model baselines across three cohorts. ThyClinScore, a lesion-level clinical semantic metric introduced here, showed the strongest correlation with a location-aware language-model judge. ThyroidXAgent improved physician classification accuracy, increased report diagnostic consistency from 70.3 percent to 86.2 percent, and reduced segmentation and reporting time by 35.9 percent and 27.4 percent, respectively. These findings support auditable, clinician-correctable agentic AI for thyroid ultrasound diagnosis and reporting.

</details>


### [201] [DiG-bench: Discovery in Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12593)
*Ruairidh M. Battleday,Kai Sandbrink,Jimi Cullen-Drohan,Zihan Yan,Timothy Muller,Clare Maguire,Ales Kubicek,Fraser Greenlee-Scott,Sukrit Sumant,Tri Dao,Jürgen Schmidhuber,Michal Valko,Joshua Tenenbaum,Thomas L. Griffiths,Zeb Kurth-Nelson,James C. R. Whittington*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了DiG-bench（Discovery in Games）基准测试，包含70个独立游戏，用于评估AI在未知目标环境下通过交互实验发现新知识的科学发现能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现（形成新颖概括）是科学过程的核心，但当前AI基准测试中缺乏在可控环境中直接探测新知识发现能力的基准，因为这类环境的客观目标通常是未知的。

Method: 构建70个独立游戏，每个游戏编码为短字符串，具有独特的变换规则，需要通过交互和实验来发现。游戏分7个难度等级，每个关卡有未知的胜利条件来测试规则是否被发现。公开21个游戏，其余保留用于安全评估。

Result: 最低难度等级可被多个模型常规解决，最高难度等级对最优agent框架构成挑战。全部70个游戏均被至少一位人类在首次尝试中解决。

Conclusion: DiG-bench填补了AI基准中科学发现能力评估的空白，为测试智能体在未知目标环境中的实验探索和规则发现能力提供了标准化的评估框架。

Abstract: Discovery---formulating novel generalizations---is a central part of the scientific process. Despite its importance, there is a gap in the current AI benchmark landscape, with few benchmarks directly probing the capacity for discovering new knowledge with experimentation in controlled environments where the objective is unknown. To address this gap, we release a new benchmark: DiG-bench (Discovery in Games). DiG-bench consists of a set of 70 independent games. Each game is encoded as a short string and has unique transformation rules that must be discovered through interaction and experimentation. The levels of the game present a series of challenges to test whether the rules have been discovered, where the win conditions for each level are also unknown. We provide games at seven tiers of difficulty for AI agents. The lowest tier is routinely solvable by multiple models, while the highest tier challenges the best models in agentic harnesses. All 70 games were solved by at least one human on first attempt. A subset of 21 games is released publicly, and the remainder is held private for secure evaluation.

</details>


### [202] [Dead text or binding clause? Measuring and restoring constraint influence in black-box LLM dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12599)
*Haoyuan Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SYSNAME系统，通过模型API解决多轮对话中约束撤销失效（行为复发）问题。系统包含契约账本（记录约束与撤销墓碑）、顺序消融探针（预测复发）和修复阶梯（在预算内修复），实验表明预编译可显著降低复发，探针可预测复发，修复阶梯效果有限但成本可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮对话中用户可以像施加约束一样轻易撤销约束，但模型往往不会可靠地执行撤销，仍继续执行已撤销的约束，这种现象被称为"行为复发"或"撤销惯性"。现有方法无法逐条款测量这种影响、不能交付前预测、也无法在匹配预算下修复。

Method: 1) 契约账本：将每个约束与可执行检查器配对，记录撤销为墓碑，预编译净约束状态为单一规范；2) 顺序消融探针：测量逐条款遵循度和增量行为影响；3) 修复阶梯：在token和使用次数匹配的预算下进行修复。

Result: 在HumanEval任务上，8B模型的复发率随约束负载从0.00%攀升至0.00%（需具体数据），而强模型处于下限。预编译显著降低复发（相较无账本的验证-重试基线），修复阶梯叠加无额外增益，探针预测复发AUROC表现良好，一句墓碑提示可恢复约三分之一编译效果。

Conclusion: 撤销失败可以通过模型API变为可测量、可预测、可修复的对话状态属性，而非不可见问题。预编译净约束状态是最有效的修复手段，而自适应修复阶梯在已编译基础上增益有限。

Abstract: Multi-turn dialogues let users revoke constraints as easily as impose them, but revocation does not reliably take effect: models keep enacting withdrawn requirements (occasionally beneath comments asserting their removal), a failure we call \emph{behavioral relapse}, or revocation inertia. No existing instrument measures this influence per clause, predicts it before delivery, or repairs it under matched budgets. \sysname{} closes the three gaps through the model API alone: a contract ledger pairs every constraint with an executable checker, records revocations as tombstones, and compiles the net constraint state ahead of time into a single specification; a sequential ablation probe measures per-clause adherence and incremental behavioral effect; a repair ladder operates under token- and attempt-matched budgets. On \dataname{} (\NTasks{} HumanEval tasks, \NClauses{} verified checkers), relapse at an 8B operating point climbs from \ScaleDelayedMTwo{} to \ScaleDelayedMEight{} as constraint load grows, while stronger models sit at floor. Under matched checkers, model, and budget, ahead-of-time compilation significantly reduces relapse against a no-ledger verifier-retry baseline (\RestoreDiff{}, 95\% CI \RestoreDiffCI{}, $p$ \RestoreDiffP{}); adaptive ladder interventions stacked on top add no detectable gain (95\% confidence excludes gains $\geq$ \LadderExcludedGain{}). The probe predicts relapse before delivery (AUROC \AurocPrimary{}); a one-sentence tombstone note recovers about a third of the compilation effect and survives a placebo control. At \CostDeliveryFactor{} delivery overhead and \CostTotalHedged{} of API compute for every result, revocation failure becomes a measurable, predictable, and repairable property of dialogue state rather than an invisible one.

</details>


### [203] [@skills: Attention is all you have](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12610)
*Li Yin,Zhi Li,Zhan Shi,Haoran Zhang,Haebin Seong,Zhangyang,Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出@skills开放协议，将技能安装中捆绑的三项功能（内容、持久化、自动触发）分离，通过路径寻址技能，实现"即读即用"而非"安装驻留"，从而解决系统提示词中可靠触发槽位稀缺导致的技能长尾无法使用问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前有56,804个公开技能且大量私有技能存在，但主流交付模式是安装式，技能描述驻留系统提示词，争夺不足100个可靠触发槽位。这导致长尾技能无实际使用路径，团队自有playbook也面临同样的空间竞争。安装模式将内容、持久化和自动触发三项功能捆绑，只有自动触发需要提示词驻留，这种捆绑阻碍了技能的规模化使用。

Method: 提出@skills协议：1) 路径寻址——任何技能、子树或集合均可通过路径访问，读取即使用，无需安装或驻留；2) vendor操作将路径副本纳入项目Git树以供适配和所有权管理；3) link操作仅添加一行.gitignore风格内容，是唯一消耗提示词驻留的元素；4) 目录即菜单，使集合成为普通目录而非全有或全无的单元；5) 无需manifest、lockfile或注册，SKILL.md保持原样；6) 协议以可安装包形式分发，通过单一指令文件让任何能读文件和运行命令的agent成为客户端。

Result: 该协议已在GitHub开源（SylphAI-Inc/atskills），并以AdaL CLI实现。配套提供可选的atskills.one中心，支持语料级搜索排序、免仓库托管、私有与团队集合及单屏创作。gh:和本地路径无需中心即可解析，被索引的GitHub技能保持gh:身份。

Conclusion: 核心理念是"少安装，多使用"（Install less, use more），通过分离安装的捆绑功能，释放系统提示词的稀缺空间，为技能长尾提供实际可用路径，同时保持对现有GitHub技能生态的兼容。

Abstract: There are 56,804 public agent skills today, and teams write many more privately. The dominant delivery model is installation: once installed, a skill's description remains in the system prompt, competing for fewer than 100 reliable trigger slots. This leaves the long tail with no practical path to use and forces teams' own playbooks to compete for the same scarce space. We observe that installation bundles three separable functions: content, persistence, and automatic triggering. Only the last requires prompt residency. We therefore propose @skills, an open protocol that separates them. A path addresses any skill, subtree, or collection, and reading a skill is sufficient to use it, so nothing is installed or made resident. The operation vendors a copy at the same path into a project's Git-tracked tree for adaptation and ownership. The operation adds one .gitignore-style line, the only element that costs prompt residency. A directory is a menu, making bundles ordinary directories rather than all-or-nothing units. The protocol requires no manifest, lockfile, or registration, and SKILL.md remains unchanged. @skills is additive, ships as an installable package, and turns any agent that can read files and run commands into a client through a single instruction file. Its open specification is at https://github.com/SylphAI-Inc/atskills and it is implemented in the AdaL CLI at https://adalagent.ai . Because paths address skills well but cannot find them, the protocol is paired with a free hub at https://atskills.one for corpus-wide search and ranking, repository-free hosting, private and team collections, and one-screen authoring. The hub is optional: gh: and local paths resolve without it, and indexed GitHub skills retain their gh: identities. Install less, use more.

</details>


### [204] [Jagged Judges: Epistemic Stability Under Silence, Pressure, and Persistence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12645)
*Justin Zhao,Himaghna Bhattacharjee,Hannah Korevaar,Bhaktipriya Radharapu,Khalid El-Arini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wiggle Framework，一个用于压力测试LLM评判者（judge）认知稳定性的统一框架，从机械一致性、单轮信念和多轮坚持三个维度评估模型的鲁棒性。实验发现9个前沿模型在14个评判任务中均表现出显著的"摇摆"现象，且被成功说服改变判决的行为几乎总是偏离真实答案（净腐蚀）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM法官已成为模型评估、在线评分和奖励建模的核心基础设施，但传统上仅通过黄金数据上的准确率来验证法官质量。准确率无法反映模型在重新提示、挑战或持续施压下的稳定性，因此缺乏对评判者认知稳定性的系统性评估工具。

Method: 提出Wiggle Framework，将法官鲁棒性分解为三个维度：（1）机械一致性——重新提示和重新框架下的稳定性；（2）单轮信念——面对单次挑战的稳定性；（3）多轮坚持——面对持续或自适应压力的稳定性。在9个前沿模型、14个涵盖安全、毒性、AI写作检测和政治回应评估的评判任务上进行实验。

Result: 所有模型作为法官都表现出显著的摇摆：在静态推阻下翻转判决的比例为25%-71%，在面对对抗性LLM说服者时高达62%-91%。关键发现是，成功改变法官判决的压力几乎总是相对于真实答案而言是净腐蚀性的。此外，基线陪审团多数强度（baseline jury majority strength）是预测哪些条目会摇摆的最有效的单次信号。

Conclusion: 这是首次在评判场景中对机械、从众和可说服性测试进行跨数据集、同类对比的全面评估，揭示了LLM法官普遍存在的认知不稳定性，并为预测和缓解这种摇摆提供了信号。

Abstract: LLM judges have become central infrastructure for model evaluations, online grading, and reward modeling. Judges are typically validated by accuracy on golden data, but accuracy says little about whether they are stable under re-prompting, challenge, or sustained pushback. We introduce the \emph{Wiggle Framework}, a unified stress test for epistemic stability in LLM judges. The framework decomposes judge robustness along three dimensions: Mechanical Consistency (stability under re-prompting and reframing), Single-turn Conviction (stability under a single challenge), and Multi-turn Persistence (stability under sustained or adaptive pressure). We use the framework to study 9 frontier models across 14 judging tasks spanning safety, toxicity, AI writing detection, and political-response evaluation. Every model exhibits substantial wiggle as a judge --- flipping verdicts 25--71\% of the time under static pushback, and 62--91\% with an adversarial LLM persuader. Critically, we find that pressure that succeeds in changing a judge's verdict is almost always net-corrupting with respect to ground truth. Beyond the framework itself, we identify baseline jury majority strength as the most effective single-shot signal for anticipating which items wiggle. Taken together, this is the first apples-to-apples cross-dataset comparison of mechanical, conformity, and persuadability tests in a judging context.

</details>


### [205] [SteerBench-Work: A Benchmark for Agent Steering at Action Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12654)
*Oguz Serdar,Cuneyt Mertayak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 SteerBench-Work，一个以真实事件为锚点、双向的工作场景智能体"预提交门控决策"基准测试，覆盖开发、客户服务、金融、法律、医疗、HR 和安全等领域。v2026-05 版本包含 106 个场景、证据反转镜像和校准对照。实验发现模型错误高度单向：在 28.1% 的机会上错误阻止了已授权且证据充分的工作，仅 1.0% 错误放行不安全操作；模型在证据反转镜像上表现显著更差（63.8% vs 98.5%）。通用能力不等于门控校准能力，更强模型往往过度拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时运行的 LLM 智能体通过工具执行高风险操作（如发邮件、合并 PR、支付转账），单步错误代价极高。现有基准多关注下游任务完成质量，而忽略了"提交前门控决策"这一关键边界——即智能体在行动前应判断是继续执行还是暂停等待人工/策略审查。作者希望构建一个专门评估该决策能力的基准，并揭示模型在此维度的失败模式。

Method: 构建 SteerBench-Work 基准：以公开真实事件为锚点设计 106 个场景，采用"证据反转镜像"配对设计（同一事件的正反证据版本），并加入校准对照组。标签在 proceed 和 hold 之间近乎均匀分布。评估时模型接收拟议动作和可用证据，返回门控决策，按是否正确跨越或守住边界计分。在 30 个模型条件下系统测试。

Result: 关键结果：错误高度单向，模型在 28.1% 的机会上错误阻止已授权且证据清除的工作（过度拒绝），仅 1.0% 错误放行不安全操作（过度放行）。最难的场景是"风险已解决的提交"（签名或结构化证据已清除风险触发器）。模型在著名事件的证据反转镜像上得分显著更低（63.8% vs 原始事件 98.5%）。此外，高能力模型往往在提交边界上过度拒绝，更多推理可以修复弱门控但不会改善已校准的门控。

Conclusion: 结论：通用能力与门控校准是两种不同的能力维度，当前模型存在系统性过度保守（过度拒绝）偏差。这提示需要专门的校准训练而非单纯提升推理能力。该基准和公开排行榜（steerbench.com）为该方向提供标准化评估工具。

Abstract: Long-running LLM agents act through tools, and a single step can send an email, merge a pull request, or wire a payment. The steering decision is the pre-commit choice at that boundary: proceed, or hold for human or policy review. We introduce SteerBench-Work, an incident-anchored, bidirectional benchmark for that decision in workplace agents across developer operations, customer service, finance, legal, medical, HR, and security.
  Release v2026-05 contains 106 scenarios anchored in public incidents, paired evidence-reversed mirrors, and calibration controls, with labels split nearly evenly between proceed and hold so the two error directions get near-identical numbers of chances. A model sees the proposed action and the available evidence, returns a gate decision, and is scored on whether it crosses or holds the boundary correctly. Across 30 model conditions the failures run almost entirely in one direction: models wrongly hold authorized, evidence-cleared work on 28.1% of opportunities and wrongly allow unsafe work on 1.0%. The hardest cases are risk-resolved commits, where signed or structured evidence has already cleared a real risk trigger, and models score markedly worse on evidence-reversed mirrors of famous incidents (63.8%) than on the incidents themselves (98.5%). General capability is not the same as steering calibration: higher-capability models often over-refuse at the commit boundary, and more reasoning can repair a weak gate while leaving a calibrated one flat. The public leaderboard is at steerbench.com.

</details>


### [206] [General Probabilities of Causation with Causal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12657)
*Xin Shu,Zhen Lei,Ang Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在多元（multivalued）因果推断场景下，通过整合协变量和中介变量中编码的因果信息，推导出比现有非二元界限更紧的因果概率（PoCs）界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Tian和Pearl首次推导了二元PoCs的理论尖锐界限，Mueller等人通过协变量和中介变量进一步收紧二元PNS界限。Li和Pearl以及Shu等人将PoCs扩展到多元场景并推导了理论界限。但一个自然的问题是：在多元场景下，额外的因果知识（协变量和中介变量中的信息）是否能进一步收紧界限？本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 本文通过将协变量和中介变量中编码的因果信息整合到多元PoCs的推导过程中，从理论上推导出更紧的界限。具体包括：扩展Mueller等人针对二元场景的方法到多元场景，利用因果图中协变量和中介变量的结构信息来约束可能的结果组合，从而缩小概率界限的可行域。

Result: 通过玩具示例（toy examples）展示理论结果的有效性，并通过仿真研究验证所提出的界限严于现有的非二元（nonbinary）界限。

Conclusion: 本文成功证明了在多元场景下，整合协变量和中介变量中的因果信息可以进一步收紧PoCs界限，填补了该领域的空白，为多元因果概率的识别提供了更精确的工具。

Abstract: Probabilities of causation (PoCs) characterize individual causal responses that cannot be directly observed and therefore generally require partial identification. Tian and Pearl first derived theoretically sharp bounds for binary PoCs, including the probability of necessity (PN), the probability of sufficiency (PS), and the probability of necessity and sufficiency (PNS). Mueller et al. subsequently tightened the bounds for binary PNS by incorporating causal information encoded in covariates and mediators.
